## Known drift (open)

- **Brand palette fails WCAG AA contrast (site-wide, found 2026-08-19)**: `--color-text-muted: #718096` measures **4.01:1 on white**, 3.83:1 on `#f7fafc`, 3.57:1 on `#eef2f8` — AA needs 4.5:1 at body size, so it fails on every surface. The `#667eea` link colour measures **3.66:1 on white**, 3.49:1 on `#f7fafc`. Fixed **only in `css/fundamentals.css`** (muted -> `#4a5568`, new `--color-link: #4c51bf`); the tokens are unchanged everywhere else, so other pages very likely carry the same failure and pass their audits only because they don't hit these combinations at body size. Repainting the brand palette is an owner call — not done.
- **`libraries.html` failed WCAG AA (found 2026-08-19, FIXED 2026-09-01)**: `--acc` `#0EA5E9` measured **2.58:1** on `--bg` as text (`.eyebrow`, `.count-chip`, `.lib-c`, `.lib-go`), and `--faint` 4.38:1 light / 3.99:1 on a dark card (`.lib-grp-sub`). Fixed as memory predicted: a separate `--acc-ink` (`#0369A1` light, `#38BDF8` dark) for the text, leaving `--acc` on the icon fill and hover border it also paints; `--faint` to `#626F82` / `#7A88A0`. Worst pair now 4.75:1. The `<main>` landmark also opened below the hero, so "Skip to content" jumped past the h1 — moved above it. **The lesson is why it shipped, not the colours**: `libraries.html` was in none of the audit page lists, so every run was green while the page carried 29 failing nodes in light and 4 in dark. Added to `tests/axe-accessibility.js`; still absent from `.pa11yci` and `scripts/mobile/audit.py`, which remain hand-enumerated. **Third instance of the brand-palette contrast defect — the token-level fix is still not done.**
- **Two CI jobs stall instead of failing (found 2026-08-19)**: `Mobile overflow audit (390px)` and `Check broken links` hung `in_progress` 25+ min on PR #935 and #936 with logs returning 404 (they die before any test body runs; the mobile job does `playwright install --with-deps`). Both completed normally on #937 (2:58, 3:45), so it is a transient runner failure, not the code. A check that hangs rather than fails is one nobody can rely on. **RESOLVED 2026-08-20**: every job in `ci.yml` now carries `timeout-minutes`, so a stall goes red instead of reading as "still running".
- **Three CI audits enumerate pages by hand, so new pages silently escape them (found 2026-08-19)**: `tests/axe-accessibility.js`, `.pa11yci` and `scripts/mobile/audit.py` each run against a fixed list. `fundamentals.html` was in none of them, so all three reported "All pages passed" while the page carried **34 serious axe violations**. Added it to all three (axe 5->6, pa11y 13->14, mobile 28->29), but the pattern repeats for every new page. Consider a glob, or a guard that fails when a new root-level HTML file is absent from the lists. **PARTLY ADDRESSED 2026-08-20**: `check-viewport.py` and `check-search-coverage.py` both enumerate by glob/sitemap rather than by hand, so new pages are covered automatically — but `tests/axe-accessibility.js`, `.pa11yci` and `scripts/mobile/audit.py` still use fixed lists and still have this hole. **2026-09-01 — the list was only half the problem.** Being *in* the list does not mean a page is audited. `tests/axe-accessibility.js` marked a page that failed to load as SKIPPED and still exited 0; only a wholly unreachable site failed. Measured on this repo: **14 of 44 page/variant runs audited, 30 skipped, verdict PASSED.** Two causes, both fixed: it waited for network idle, which never arrives when a page pulls assets from a CDN that is slow or blocked (`libraries.html` requests 16 jsdelivr icons and left nine in flight forever) — it now falls back to `domcontentloaded` and audits the page; and a page that no longer existed loaded the server's **404 body**, audited it, and reported PASS, so a renamed page could sit in the list passing indefinitely — the HTTP status is now asserted. Any page in the list that could not be audited now fails the run. Verified after: **44 of 44 audited** (39 via the fallback), zero violations — nothing had been hiding behind the 30 skips. **`.pa11yci` and `scripts/mobile/audit.py` have not been checked for the same hole.**

- **21 real-emoji chars across 7 files** (📚📧💬👋♥🌐📩⚠📏🏆🎓🗓): functional UI category icons in chatbot menus (`accessibility.html`, `forgot-password.html`), friendly greetings (`👋` on `blog.html`, `catalog.html`), and content emphasis (`blog/whats-coming-in-2026.html` 3, `content-marketing-kit.html` 5, `blog/sample-size-matters.html` 2). Need bespoke 1:1 SVG mapping per use case rather than bulk substitution — deferred to a dedicated emoji-to-Sargam pass.
- **Wiki sync round 3**: ~4 of 11 wiki pages still stale after May 1 rounds 1+2 (Architecture, Premium-and-Business-Models, plus 2 more).
- **pa11y baseline cleanup (issue #360)**: ~410 errors remain on lexicons, handouts, premium, dataverse pages.
- **Pubchoice NotebookLM URL**: pending — needs user to create the notebook in Google NotebookLM and share the URL for `data/notebooklm-registry.json`. After this, all 12 flagships will have AI Companions.

- **2026-08-21 — Mapped instructor kits live in Notion, not in the repo (owner directive)**: The `instructor-kit` form on `teach.html` promises a kit **mapped to that instructor's own course**, so there is deliberately **no general kit page on the site** — `instructor-kit.html` was built, then deleted, and 301s to `/teach.html#kit`. Each kit is now written per request in a private Notion workspace, **ImpactMojo Teaching Kits** (home page `3c347f92-ed4b-8105-9f8c-c34af10b150c`; `Kits` database, data source `d9d62ef3-2df5-43dd-9edc-57c7bf5bc1f3`, columns Instructor/Institution/Email/Course/Level/Status/Requested/Sent/Gaps flagged; duplicate the TEMPLATE row `3c347f92-ed4b-81de-9695-fcde337e6423`). **Sharing: publish the individual kit page to web with search-engine indexing OFF, and send that `.notion.site` link — never publish the Kits database** (it holds every instructor's email and every gap we have admitted to). Per-page publish was chosen over inviting guests precisely so an instructor is not forced to open a Notion account. Each kit must state **near the top** what ImpactMojo does *not* cover for that person, must not pad the mapping to look complete, and must check `data/challenges.json` before calling any challenge free. **Register: no epigrams, no "that is where the thinking is" constructions, no "reading spine"/"workbench"/"mission-aligned" vocabulary** — the owner rejected these three separate times across the session; headings should say what the thing is. First kit drafted for Pankaj Das (`3c347f92-ed4b-81ba-a933-fd43d886741e`, Status **Drafted**, not yet sent) — he teaches Sustainability/ESG/CSR/M&E and we can only map the M&E half, which the kit says in its opening lines.
- **2026-08-21 — Nothing about a partner, or about ImpactMojo's own practice, goes on a page unless the owner said it or a source says it (learned the hard way)**: `partnerships.html` shipped with invented partnership activities for Populi and India Institute and an invented partnership policy, all of it plausible and none of it true. Where a partner's own words are used, quote them and attribute them to their site so the reader can see whose claim it is. Where there is nothing to say, say less — a short page is correct; filler is not. This subsumes the 2026-07-19 "no tech jargon" and 2026-08-19 "AI language" directives into their common cause: **an elevated register demands claims, and absent facts it invents them.** Default to plain declarative prose in user-facing copy, so missing information reads as missing.
- **2026-07-19 — No tech jargon in user-facing copy (owner directive)**: sitewide sweep removed implementation language (Supabase/GA4/axe-core/pa11y/service worker/localStorage/API/self-hosted fonts) from VISIBLE reading text, rewritten to user benefit or deleted. Two traps worth remembering: (a) the essay's body said "is the fourth essay in the Marginalia series" but slots 4 and 5 had been taken since June, so merging as-is would have published a factual error in the author's voice — always re-check a stale branch's claims about *the site's own state*; (b) applying JSON-oriented i18n checks to markdown produces false positives — "cross-script leakage" was a language list using endonyms (హిందీ (हिन्दी)) and "repetition runs" were box-drawing characters in ASCII diagrams.
- **2026-08-15 — the LinkedIn handle is `learn-impactmojo`, not `impactmojo`**: the old URL 404s and was wrong on **35 site files** (footers, legal pages, press kit, sitemap, JSON-LD `sameAs`). Correct: `https://www.linkedin.com/company/learn-impactmojo/`. Two lessons. (1) LinkedIn returns 999/403 to plain automated requests, so a link checker cannot tell "blocked" from "gone" — re-probe with a browser User-Agent, which separated them cleanly here (new 200 / old 404). (2) When doing a sitewide URL replace, **exclude CHANGELOG.md and memory.md**: they quote broken URLs *as* broken, and rewriting them silently falsifies the record. Caught this one only because a follow-up assertion failed.
- **2026-08-15 — dead citations: several were WRONG, not just moved (#924)**: researching the 25 left over from #922 resolved 11, and the pattern is worth knowing — a dead citation link is often evidence the citation itself is wrong. *Evidence-Based Policy* was linked to Cambridge but is Oxford (the page's own text said "Oxford, 2012"); *Modi's India* to Harvard but is Princeton; *Republic of Caste* to Penguin but is Navayana; the journal *Public Choice* to Cambridge Core but is Springer; the SuperAmma trial carried DOI `S2214-109X(14)70160-8` when the real one is `(13)70160-8`; Jones & Bouffard carried an *Educational Psychologist* DOI when the article is SRCD *Social Policy Report* 26(4) 2012. Method that worked: read the anchor text to identify the actual work, search for its canonical page, curl-verify, and only then swap. **14 still open** (J-PAL handbook/poor-economics/podcast, WHO 9241591552, ACER, Brookings SEL, Blavatnik DDD, dochas, fair.work India, rice institute, WID billionaire raj, DostEducation, Spears/AEJ, and the site's own LinkedIn) — listed in PR #922. Remember: OUP, Wiley, Cambridge, Harvard and Princeton return **403 to bots**, so a 403 is never evidence of a dead link.
- **2026-08-15 — branch deletion is only possible via a workflow, not from a session (#923)**: the agent proxy blocks every repo-scoped GitHub write from a Claude Code session — `git push --delete`, a direct `$GITHUB_PAT` push, and REST `DELETE /git/refs` all return the proxy's own 403 body ("GitHub access is not enabled for this session. An org admin must connect the Claude GitHub App"). `GET /user` works, so the PAT authenticates; it is the repo scope that is blocked, and `X-Oauth-Scopes` comes back empty because the request never reaches GitHub's auth layer. The GitHub MCP server is the only sanctioned repo channel and has `create_branch` but no delete. A pull request cannot delete other branches either. **The answer is a `workflow_dispatch` workflow** — it runs on GitHub's runners with its own GITHUB_TOKEN and `contents: write`, so the proxy is irrelevant. `.github/workflows/delete-stale-branches.yml` + `.github/stale-branches.txt` (46 entries, reviewable in git) do this: dry-run unless the operator types DELETE, refuses default/protected branches, and re-queries open PRs at run time so a stale audit cannot cause a bad delete. Same pattern applies to any future repo-write task blocked from a session.

Notices live on nas/nfhs/plfs/udise + dataverse-india; each "still shows X" half was checked against the page. **Dead links** (#922): scanning the 40 files the external-links routines touched found **30 real 404s and 30 × 403 that are publisher bot-blocks, not breakage** — never "fix" the 403s. Five repaired with verified 200s (BSC→Harvard HKS, PRS billtrack→Act 29 of 2023 PDF, two J-PAL deep links→/research-resources, MHRD NEP PDF→/nep/nep-2020); the other 25 documented in the PR rather than guessed, because a wrong publisher/DOI target is a mis-citation and worse than a 404. **Open for the owner**: `linkedin.com/company/impactmojo` on catalog.html 404s (may be LinkedIn bot-blocking — only the owner can confirm the handle). search-index.json needed no change: its explorer descriptions state what each page contains, not that it is the latest round.

Pattern to use is `<p style="display:none"><label>Don't fill: <input name="bot-field"></label></p>`. (2) **Structured data**: Dataset/LearningResource JSON-LD on 25 pages + og:image on 10 explorers. Built `status.html` — a **client-side** health-check page (no backend service required), matching JanVayu's "probed live from your browser" model. **Probes 11 components** in two groups: *Website & Pages* (homepage, catalog, game-library, handouts, blog, docs) + *Data & APIs* (search-index.json, version.json/CDN, `/.netlify/functions/translate`, Supabase `auth/v1/health`, GitHub API). **Probe types**: `origin` (same-origin pages, HEAD → status+latency), `json` (GET + parse to validate), `fn` (any non-5xx = up — used for translate which 405s on GET, AND for GitHub because the unauthenticated API rate-limits to 60/hr and auto-refresh hits ~60/hr so 403s must NOT read as down), `cors` (res.ok — Supabase health). **Behaviour**: auto-refresh every 60s, 10s AbortController timeout → down, >2s → degraded; colour-coded overall banner (Operational/Degraded/Partial-Major Outage); manual "Refresh now"; last-checked time. **90-day uptime strip** is per-browser from `localStorage` (key `imx-status-history-v1`, stores worst daily observation, prunes >120d) — labelled "this browser" since client-side can't do cross-visitor history. Full brand template (header/footer/theme-toggle/GA/fonts/skip-link/paper-plane/auth scripts, absolute URLs). **Cross-refs updated**: `sitemap.xml` (priority 0.5, changefreq always), changelog v10.63.0 (For Learners + Added), footer Resources link on the page itself. (2) **#557** — cookie consent consolidated into a single shared `css/cookie.css` (was duplicated inline across pages). (3) **#558** — `about.html` had an unterminated-string JS error breaking its theme toggle / mobile menu / cookie-expand; fixed. (4) **#556** — CI GitHub-Action version bumps (Node 24) + added `updates.html` to `data/search-index.json` (pre-existing gap); also merged Dependabot axios+qs and closed #343/#344. (5) **THEME SWEEP #559/#560/#561** — consolidated ~**304 of 329** pages onto a single shared `js/theme.js` (a behavioural superset of ~18 divergent inline theme variants; handles `.im-theme-btn`+`.theme-btn`, all four localStorage keys, and sets BOTH `data-theme` attr AND `body.dark-mode`/`.light-mode`). Done in 3 installments: 3a=67 self-contained pages, 3b=219 more, 3c=18 *interleaved* pages (theme code mixed with chatbot/menu in one `<script>`). **3c safety method** (reusable): removed the contiguous theme region + leftover standalone re-apply / `DOMContentLoaded` init statements, then GATED each page on BOTH `node --check` (syntax) AND a zero-residual-reference scan (no inline code may still call `applyTheme`/`getPreferredTheme`/`updateButtons`/`getSystemTheme`); pages failing either gate auto-reverted. Caught a real regex-boundary bug (internal paren in `'(prefers-color-scheme: dark)'` truncated the region) before it shipped. (6) **#562 — Phase 4: z-index scale** — replaced 26 scattered z-index magic numbers in `css/imx-main.css` with a documented named ladder of CSS custom properties in `:root` (`--z-rail` 900 … `--z-menu-btn-max` 99999). Rendering-neutral by construction (each literal → token whose VALUE equals it; word-boundary regex so 1000≠10001); verified in headless Chromium that all 12 tokens resolve to exact original values and computed z-index of `.header`/`.imx-speed-dial` is unchanged. **KEY FINDING on the `!important` "debt"**: the audit framed Phase 4 as removing ~548 `!important`, but analysis showed that premise is largely WRONG — **399/530 (75%) are inside `@media` blocks** (load-bearing responsive overrides; removing breaks mobile layout) and most of the 131 base-level ones override `premium.js` inline styles (~33), the third-party **UserWay** widget, or the V3 redesign forcing over original CSS. A blind sweep is unsafe. The correct fix is a **CSS `@layer` migration** (precedence from layer order, not `!important`) — filed as **issue #563** as a deliberate, visual-regression-gated future effort, NOT a headless sweep. **Deferred & documented**: the @layer migration (#563); the ~29 bespoke/reverted theme pages still on inline code (`ImpactMojo_PressKit` with its own `.tb`/`im-t` system, the 10 Labs, catalog, handouts, dojos, lexicons + 9 pages whose region removal failed the safety gates). **No changelog/search-index/sitemap changes** for the CSS/JS refactors (rendering-neutral, no new content/URLs) except the #556 `updates.html` search-index backfill. **Verification harness used throughout**: local `python3 -m http.server` + Playwright headless Chromium asserting theme toggle changes `<body>` background and computed styles match pre-change values. #562 CI watcher running at session pause; merge on green.

- **2026-05-03 (PR #400 v10.23.32 — fix duplicate s121 + chart resize-on-activate)**: User flagged social-margins slides 119/120/121 all blank. Two distinct bugs: (1) **DUPLICATE s121** — both s120 and s121 contained "Manual Scavenging Deaths" with same canvas id `sm_scavenging_deaths`; ECharts can only init one canvas per id, so the second was always blank. Removed s121, renumbered End slide s122→s121, updated SLIDE_IDS array, fixed end-byline. Total slides now **121** (was 122). (2) **CHART RESIZE-ON-ACTIVATE** across all 7 native decks — ECharts canvases initialize at deck load when only the first slide is `display:flex`; canvases inside `display:none` slides have width 0 and render to 0×0. When user navigates to chart slide, canvas has dimensions but no resize event fires, so chart stays blank. Fix: MutationObserver on `.slide.active` class changes → calls `echarts.getInstanceByDom(canvas).resize()` for every chart in newly active slide. Initial 400ms pass for first slide. Known follow-ups: s117/s118 overflow (26 reading items / 4898-char glossary) need editorial trim; s2/s55/s113 "mostly blank" — need user re-check post-deploy. Branch `claude/continue-session-lyDb9` at `2349709`; main at `520be60`.
- **2026-05-03 (PR #399 v10.23.31 — proportional auto-fit rule)**: User: "No content exceeds the viewport dimensions without shrinking font too much and also without allowing overflow." Implemented across all 7 native decks: at `.slide.active` change (MutationObserver), JS measures `slide-content.scrollHeight` vs `clientHeight`. If natural > available, applies CSS `zoom = avail / natural` to scale slide-content proportionally (fonts, padding, margins all together — preserves visual hierarchy). Hard floor at **0.85x** (max 15% reduction, fonts stay ≥ ~13px). Below 0.85x, allows overflow rather than making text unreadable — signal for editorial trim. Distinct from v10.23.22's `.compact`/`.ultra-compact` which used selective font-size reductions breaking hierarchy and went to 11.5px. Browser support: Chrome/Safari/Edge always, Firefox 126+ (May 2024). Branch: `claude/continue-session-lyDb9` at `6957d13`; main at `1d32960`.
- **2026-05-03 (PR #398 v10.23.30 — slide deep-linking + climate slide 4 overflow fix)**: User asked: "make slide by slide URLs, no matter what slide I am on I see the same URL up top". Implemented across all 7 native decks: `MutationObserver` watches `.slide.active` class changes → syncs `location.hash` via `history.replaceState` (no page reload). On load (with hash) + on `hashchange` event → finds target slide, tries deck's `showSlide()`/`show()` with multiple signatures (1-indexed and 0-indexed differ across decks), falls back to manual class swap + progress UI update. Deep-link URLs like `…/climate-essentials.html#s31` now jump directly to slide 31; browser back/forward traverses slide history. Plus: climate-essentials slide 4 (Weather vs Climate) had its 4th bullet "The 2022 Pakistan floods…" clipping at the bottom now that font-shrinking is disabled — removed (same data point present in slide 9's Attribution evidence hbox with fuller context). Branch: `claude/continue-session-lyDb9` at `ad571e4`; main at `a408e45`.

Root cause: `.bullet-list li { display:flex; gap:12px }` made each `<strong>` a separate flex item from the trailing text node, creating a 2-column layout per bullet. Fix: replaced flex with `position:relative + padding-left:19px` on `<li>`, absolute-positioned `::before` bullet dot at `left:0; top:9px`. Strong + body now wrap together as one inline flow. Applied to all 7 native decks (each had different default bullet color — handled). Responsive override `margin-top:7px → top:8px` for the larger 8px bullet on bigger screens. - **2026-05-01 (Public Choice launch + perf overhaul + nav fixes)**: Major session, 26 commits to main. Highlights:
  - **Public Choice flagship** added as the 12th flagship course at `/courses/pubchoice/`. Title aligned to flagship pattern: "Public Choice: Decisions, Incentives & Institutions" (matches "Public Policy: Process, Design & Governance"). Indigo gradient card. 13 modules imported to Supabase `course_content` table — table is `course_content` not `modules` (deployment notes were wrong). Module 1 only has `is_preview=true` to match convention used by other flagships.
  - **CSS extraction perf win**: Pulled 215 KB of inline `<style>` (4 head blocks) out of `index.html` into `/css/imx-main.css`. HTML brotli wire size 96 KB → 64 KB. Cascade preserved by concatenating in document order. Body-position `<style>` blocks (5, 14 KB total) left inline because cascade-position-sensitive.
  - **HTML edge cache**: Added `[[headers]]` for `/*.html` and `/` in `netlify.toml`: `public, max-age=300, must-revalidate`. TTFB dropped from 1.4s → 175 ms (8×). Was previously `max-age=0, must-revalidate`. Auto-purge on deploy means freshness is preserved.
  - **Eliminated `on-web.link`** from all live pages. Replaced `_redirects` proxies with **Netlify Edge Functions** because `_redirects` rules can't differentiate trailing-slash forms (Netlify normalises) → caused redirect loops AND blank pages (relative asset paths broke). Edge functions proxy `varnasr.github.io/PolicyDhara` and `varnasr.github.io/development-discourses`, inject a `<base href>` into HTML so relative paths resolve. Updated 4 pages that linked to old `on-web.link/DevDiscourses`.
  - **Specials nav redesigned** as 4-section accordion (Reference Libraries / Long-form Reading / Practice & Programs / Behind the Scenes), all collapsed by default, single-open behaviour. CSS appended to extracted `imx-main.css`. Mobile cap (`max-height: 500px`) was clipping accordion items — lifted for `.specials-grouped` specifically.
  - **Navigation bug chain solved**: ALL Specials links now absolute (`/#flagship-courses`, `/#case-studies`, `/#dev-discourses`, `/dataverse.html`, `/challenges.html`). The actual blocker was **`js/router.js` matching path `/` to the `home` route and overriding the hash** — fixed by checking `window.location.hash` first in `handleRoute()`. Hash always wins over path-based default route. Same fix benefits all hash-based nav.
  - **Mobile margin safety net** appended to `imx-main.css`: forces `padding-left/right: 1.25rem` on top-level sections, named `imx-*` sections, and id'd sections. Tightens to 1rem at ≤380px. Card grids collapse to single column. Worst offender was `.imx-libraries-grid-section` (was `padding: 2rem 0`).
  - **Public Choice hero**: 3 inline `color: rgba(255,255,255,...)` elements were invisible on light bg. Switched to `var(--color-text-secondary)` / `var(--color-text)`. Boundary callout strong/link wrapped in `.pubchoice-boundary-strong` class with theme-aware amber (#92400E light / #FCD34D dark).
  - **DevEcon CSS shim**: `var(--indigo)`, `--cyan`, `--orange`, `--success` referenced 17 times but never defined. Added to all 4 :root / theme blocks. Same bug pattern that was fixed in pubchoice earlier.
  - **Catalog WCAG fix**: `.track-filter.active` was sky-500 on sky-500-at-20% (~2.8:1, fails AA). Switched to amber-700 light / sky-300 dark.
  - **`js/faq-bank.js` line 167** had stray `""` mid-string — killed file parsing. Fixed.
  - **PolicyDhara link** updated from `https://on-web.link/PolicyDhara` to `https://www.impactmojo.in/policydhara` on the homepage card. Also changed `#policy-dhara` anchor in nav to `/policydhara`.
  - **Bylines for Deep Dives**: Sukhmeet Bedi role corrected from "Founder, ImpactMojo" to "Series Editor — Deep Dives" (user is the founder, not Sukhmeet). 3 of 5 Deep Dives now bylined "ImpactMojo Editorial / House Pick" instead of all-Sukhmeet.
  - **Key reusable learnings:**
    - Netlify `_redirects` normalises trailing slashes when matching → can't reliably differentiate `/foo` from `/foo/` for proxy rules. Use Edge Functions when you need exact-form matching.
    - When proxying a third-party HTML page through Netlify, inject `<base href="/your-path/">` into the HTML or its relative asset URLs will resolve against the wrong base and the page will render unstyled.
    - Site-wide JS routers can override browser-native hash navigation. Always check `window.location.hash` BEFORE matching path → route in any client router.
    - Capture-phase click handlers + `stopPropagation` will block bubble-phase mobile-menu-close handlers. If you need to force navigation on capture, also explicitly close any UI that's expected to dismiss on click.
    - Inline `style="color: rgba(255,255,255,...)"` on hero text is the most common dark-mode-residue bug — pages designed for a dark hero get re-skinned to light without removing white text.
    - When extracting inline `<style>` to external file, preserve document order. Brace-balance check is a fast sanity test.
    - 215 KB CSS extraction also saves brotli bytes (96 KB → 64 KB) and lets browsers parallelise download.

Brand-aligned: Sargam icons (no emojis), Amaranth body / Inter headings, paper plane SVG, skip link. ## Known Issues

<!-- Track recurring problems and workarounds -->
- **`site-chrome.js` deletes any body-level `<header>` — ten live pages lose their `<h1>`**: the kill-list strips `.foot/.footer/footer/.site-footer` *and* any `<header>`/`<nav>` that is a direct child of `<body>`, so a `<header class="hero">` is removed at runtime with the headline inside it. Measured in Chromium 2026-08-21: `ai-agents-for-evaluators`, `ai-for-me-certificate`, `api-docs`, `build-circles`, `credential-upgrade`, `data-tech-assessed-certificate`, `events`, `mel-assessed-certificate`, `policy-economics-assessed-certificate`, `the-long-view` all have an `<h1>` in source and none in the DOM. Fix per page: make the hero a `<section>` inside `<main>`. Same trap as the 2026-07-27 `.foot` bug — recurring class, **no guard exists**; a CI check comparing source `<h1>` against rendered `<h1>` would catch the whole family.
- **Direct GitHub API via `$GITHUB_PAT` is blocked in agent sessions**: `curl https://api.github.com/...` returns `{"message": "GitHub access is not enabled for this session..."}` with no `check_runs` key. A poller that counts failures in that payload sees zero of everything and reports a **false green**. Use the `mcp__github__*` tools for PR/CI status; `$GITHUB_PAT` still works for the git push fallbacks documented in `rules/api-conventions.md`.
- `index.html` is ~620KB — always backup before major edits
- Content counts are hardcoded in 4+ locations — grep before updating
- Some `101.impactmojo.in` links are stale — migrate to self-hosted paths
- **Supabase inactivity risk mitigated**: Supabase pauses/deletes inactive free-tier projects after ~1-2 weeks. Mitigations in place:
  - `netlify/functions/daily-engagement.mjs` hits Edge Functions daily at 02:30 UTC
  - `netlify/functions/supabase-keepalive.mjs` hits Auth/DB/Storage APIs weekly at 03:17 UTC Sundays (redundancy)
  - Requires `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` env var set in Netlify dashboard
  - With both functions live, downgrading to free tier should be safe
- **Chamber petition (AOR) lives in `Varnasr/Experiments/chamber-petition/`**: Migrated from Supabase to Netlify Forms on 2026-04-16, then moved out of impactmojo/impactmojo to Varnasr/Experiments as a sandbox project. Netlify site: `chamber-petition` (id `6581a696-c2d1-4a16-bcdf-681dc517762b`) is now linked to that repo (base_dir=`chamber-petition`, deploy_key_id=`69e0d3654796c31483fe0aa4`, GitHub deploy_key=`148764262`) — pushes to Varnasr/Experiments `main` trigger auto-deploy. Form `aor-petition` (id `69e0c835009c6c0008de271a`) routes to devvrat.SCAORA@gmail.com (hook `69e0c8a0add950870abd4b78`). No standalone GitHub repo ever existed for this project. 67-signature backup preserved in the subfolder.

(b) **the tile matcher scans one line at a time**, so `<div class="stat-number">70</div>` and `<div class="stat-label">Courses</div>` on separate lines never met, and `about.html` writes every tile that way — new `cross_line_tile_hits()` matches across one line boundary in **both** orders, yielding only spans on the line being scanned, with hits de-duplicated by `(start,end)` so `--fix` cannot double-apply; (c) `101-courses/index.html` is not root-level so the glob never saw it — added to `COPY_FILES`, plus `curated tools` as a Dataverse phrase and `docs/teaching-and-lms-guide.md` + `docs/101-decks-guide.md` to `DOC_FILES`. - **2026-08-21 (b) (Data Notes + Sankey, instructor kits move to Notion, Fundamentals JS; PRs #979, #980, #981 — all merged & production-verified)**: Same day as the partnerships entry below, four separate pieces of work. **(1) PR #979 — four Data Notes on India's public money + a Sankey renderer.** Prompted by a #MakeoverMonday US-trade Sankey the owner shared. New `sankeyChart(mount, opts)` in `DataNotes/datanote.js` (now exported alongside bar/scatter/dumbbell/line): `{nodes:[{id,label,col,tone,note}], links:[{source,target,value,tone}], unit, valueFmt, height, nodeWidth, gap, minLabel, minWidth}`; node value = `max(inflow, outflow)`; one shared value→px scale across all columns. Three defects found and fixed while building, all worth knowing: labels sat on top of ribbons (→ `dv-sankey-halo`, `paint-order: stroke`), fixed 92px outer margins clipped long first/last-column labels (→ margins measured from the longest **label and value**, capped at 38% of width), and 3-column flows compressed to mush on phones (→ 460px floor for `maxCol >= 2` inside a `.dv-sankey-scroll` panning container, with `svg.style.width` pinned when panning). **`barChart` ignored `valueFmt` for axis ticks** — the dollar dive showed `+$42.45B` on bars and `45%` on the axis; fixed via `tfmt(t)`, and `tickFmt` added to `barChart` + `dumbbellChart`. Regression-checked against all 7 pre-existing dives. Dives: `all-the-growth-was-services.html` (PIB 16 Apr 2025 — services = 99.2% of export growth), `borrowing-to-pay-for-borrowing.html` (interest = 82.8% of borrowings, 18-node budget-rupee Sankey), `announced-unspent-announced-again.html` (CSS BE→RE −19.4%; MGNREGA replaced by the VB-G RAM G Act, Dec 2025), `the-41-percent-that-isnt.html` (devolution actually 34.7% of gross tax; the ₹10,909 cr remainder is labelled **"Residual"** and deliberately not attributed). Also fixed `--dv-cta-ink` (white on `#60A5FA` was 2.54:1). **(2) PR #980 — instructor-kit form + three Fundamentals pages that threw on load.** `teach.html`'s form now asks `course_name`, `level`, `course_description` (it previously captured nothing about what the person teaches, so no kit could be mapped); posts to Netlify Forms **and** `/api/form-submit`; `netlify/functions/submission-created.mjs` gained an `instructor-kit` branch sending a receipt to the requester and a brief to `ADMIN_EMAIL` (`esc()` + `looksLikeEmail()` — note `looksLikeEmail` was initially referenced before its definition and would have thrown on the first submission; caught by grep pre-merge). **`js/gender-needs.js` never called `select()` in `init()`** while both sibling modules did — the panel rendered blank; fixed with `var selected = D.cases[0].id` + `select(selected)`. `gender-needs.html`, `livelihoods.html`, `social-model.html` each loaded a stray `ladder-data.js` + `ladder.js` + init block (its own comment said "The wheel") that belonged to a different page; removed. **`capabilities.html` deliberately left alone** — it has a `#panel` and its own `capabilities.js` overwrites the ladder copy, so it renders correctly and the load order is load-bearing. **(3) PR #981 — unpublished the general instructor kit.** The form promises a kit *mapped to the requester's own course*; a public `instructor-kit.html` contradicted that, so it was deleted (also from `sitemap.xml`, `search-index.json`, and the `teach.html` link) with a 301 to `/teach.html#kit`. Four gaps the drafted kit exposed went on the roadmap under Q2 2026: **CSR & ESG 101, a Sustainability/ESG flagship, LMS-friendly exports, SCORM/xAPI**. **(4) Augmented `blog/animal-welfare-and-the-fifty-rupee-fine.html`** with a section citing Animal Ask's *Leverage Points* (1,710 → 2,473 words, 11 → 16 min). **Two accuracy traps hit and worth repeating:** practice packs are **freemium** (first 2 of 4 modules, then ₹299/pack or ₹399/mo) — I called them free after grepping a pack for "Free" and matching a *third-party tools table*; and `devecon-cost-effectiveness` is `practitioner` (paid) — **always check the tier in `data/challenges.json`** (9 explorer/free, 6 practitioner/paid) before describing a challenge as free. Also: **`/challenges/<slug>/` paths do not resolve** — those directories hold only case-packet files, and challenges render client-side from `data/challenges.json`; the internal-link guard catches this, my own "the directory exists" pre-check did not. Sandbox note: this container has a **shallow clone**, so `git pull` on `main` fails with "divergent branches" and `merge-base` finds nothing — that is the truncated history, not real divergence. A stop-hook also flags all 50 GitHub squash-merge commits as "Unverified"; they are committed by `GitHub <noreply@github.com>` / login `web-flow`, are signed by GitHub, and must **never** be rebased — the suggested `rebase --root` would rewrite all of published `main`.
- **2026-08-21 (v10.225.0–v10.226.0: partnerships.html — built, then largely un-built; PRs #977, #978)**: Owner asked for a partnerships page featuring **Populi** (populi.co.in) as end-to-end research + MEL advisory partner and **India Institute** (indiai.org) as thought advocacy + measurement partner, not linked in nav, URL to be shared when live. Built, merged (#977), verified live — **and then had to be corrected, because I had invented most of it.** The owner supplied exactly two facts (the two roles). Around them I wrote, and published as fact about two real organisations: that Populi reviews ImpactMojo's courses/labs/toolkits before release, supplies anonymised evaluation cases, and receives referrals from us; that India Institute reviews our governance and policy content; a five-point "How we work with partners" policy including **"no partner has ever been given it"** (a claim about ImpactMojo's own history, sourced from nothing) plus attribution and learner-data commitments; a six-card "Ways to partner" menu of services not offered; and form copy about how enquiries are handled. #978 strips all of it — the page drops to about a third of its length, keeping only each partner's role as given, each partner's own self-description quoted **and attributed to their site**, the service/programme lists those sites publish, and licence terms verifiable from the repo. Both changelog entries had to be corrected too: they advertised the same invented terms, so left alone they would have kept asserting them after the page stopped. **Two process failures worth carrying forward.** (1) *Register generated the content.* The marketing voice — "Open education needs practitioners behind it", "not as sponsors of a logo slot, but as…" — needs claims to fill its shape; with none available, they got manufactured. Plain declarative sentences leave the gaps visible. This is the **same** owner directive already logged on 2026-08-19 ("this has so much AI language… apply this standard to future user-facing copy") and it was not applied. (2) *I described the work as verified when the verification could not have covered it.* Contrast ratios, HTML nesting, axe-core, 31 green CI checks — none of which can tell whether a sentence is true. The PR said "every factual claim comes from their own homepage," true of the org *descriptions* and false of the *partnership* claims beside them. **Rule going forward: a claim about a third party, or about ImpactMojo's own practice, gets published only if the owner said it or a cited source says it. If neither, the page is shorter.** Technical notes from the same session: reused the **`partner-inquiry`** Netlify form name and its exact field set, since it is already on `ALLOWED_FORMS` in `netlify/functions/form-submit.mjs` (both notification and Supabase row, no backend change, same bucket as the about.html form); added the page to **`.pa11yci`** (axe-core's list is hardcoded to 10 pages in `tests/axe-accessibility.js` and does **not** cover it — pa11y's 19 do); introduced **`--accent-ink`** because `--accent` (#F59E0B) is a surface colour and was 2.05:1 as text on the light hero. **Also found, unfixed:** `site-chrome.js` deletes any body-level `<header>`, so **ten live root pages have an `<h1>` in source and none in the browser** — `ai-agents-for-evaluators`, `ai-for-me-certificate`, `api-docs`, `build-circles`, `credential-upgrade`, `data-tech-assessed-certificate`, `events`, `mel-assessed-certificate`, `policy-economics-assessed-certificate`, `the-long-view`. Measured in Chromium, not inferred. This is the **same trap already logged on 2026-07-27** for `.foot`/`footer`; it is a recurring class, not a one-off, and nothing guards it. **One tooling trap:** direct GitHub API via `$GITHUB_PAT` + curl is blocked in agent sessions (returns a JSON *error* body, not checks) — a poller counted that as "zero checks, no failures" and I reported a false green. Use the GitHub **MCP** tools for CI status.
- **2026-08-20 (v10.227.0–v10.231.0; PRs #956–#974, four GitHub Releases — the first tags since v10.79.0)**: A day of *silent failures*, most of them found by measuring rather than reading. **The big one: certificates were impossible, and finishing a course silently destroyed the completion.** `auto_issue_certificate()` called `uuid_generate_v4()`; `uuid-ossp` is installed in the `extensions` schema while the function pins `search_path` to `public,pg_temp`, so it could never resolve — and because the trigger is `BEFORE UPDATE` at 100%, its failure **rejected the write itself**. Found by signing in as a throwaway confirmed user and issuing the exact upsert the page makes (`upsert 25% OK` / `upsert 100% FAIL 404`); fixed with `gen_random_uuid()`, verified `certificate: ISSUED IM-MEL-2026-04BA79`, test user deleted, user count back to 58. Two faults it was hiding: the trigger mapped **9 course ids against the tracker's 20** (the rest returned silently uncertified), and two of the nine carried names disagreeing with the client's (`gandhi`, `poa`) so those certificates would have been publicly verifiable under a **wrong title**. Map is now complete and deliberately **server-side** — RLS lets a user write their own progress rows, so trusting `NEW.course_name` would let anyone mint a certificate for a course that does not exist. `courses/social-movements` was missing from `COURSE_NAMES` so its tracker never booted; all 19 course dirs now map. **#960 closed on this**, after its original root cause (read from static HTML; module bodies are injected at runtime from `course_content`) was publicly retracted. Also fixed: `issue-certificate` used `admin` where the client was `adminClient`, a `ReferenceError` swallowed by a catch logged "non-fatal" — **no certificate email had ever been sent** (#963). **Four new guards, each verified by reintroducing the bug**: `check-search-coverage.py` (127 live pages were in the sitemap and unfindable in site search — 47 posters, 19 companions, 17 lexicons, 8 premium tools; a stale exemption fails too), `check-viewport.py` (859 pages; testing.md had required it since the file existed with nothing checking), `check-edge-functions.py` (probes chosen so a pass proves the *function body* ran, not the gateway), `stamp-nav-dots.py` (the "recently updated" dots were hardcoded 2026-07-29 and never expired — the blog had the two newest items and no dot; they derive from content dates now and lapse after 30 days). `check-counts.py` now also scans **`js/tours.js`** — the homepage tour had drifted to 34 studios / 163 companions / 22 deep dives against 35 / 166 / 23, because the guard only ever scanned root HTML. **Edge functions deployed 2.39.3 → 2.112.3** (all five, `verify_jwt` preserved, bundles verified) and **supabase-js 2.49.1 → 2.112.3 sitewide** (509 files; the three admin pages had been on a floating `@2` and were already running 2.112.x while everything else ran 2.49.1). **#563 closed not-planned** — the `@layer` migration was attempted twice, reverted twice by the visual harness, and reaches only 2 pages. **Corrections I had to make to my own work, worth remembering:** the #960 root cause (asserted from static HTML); a claimed edge-function "drift" (I had read 258 of 423 lines); two reading companions I "fixed" for a missing viewport they already had, written unquoted past my 6KB read window; and one deploy of the *stale* source after a container rollback silently reverted the checkout. Each was caught by verifying, never by review — which is why the guards exist. **Two environment facts now corrected in `testing.md`:** the manual `service-worker.js` VERSION bump is **redundant** (Netlify's `scripts/stamp-version.sh` rewrites it to `v2-<sha>` every deploy; the repo held `v18-2026-08-20` while production served `v2-772296480539`), and Chromium **is** installed in the sandbox but **cannot reach the network** — `ERR_CONNECTION_RESET` on every external navigation — so it works against `localhost` only, which is why axe/pa11y pass and no deploy-preview test can run here. **New capability worth reusing:** `workflow_dispatch` and tag pushes are 403 from an agent session, so `release.yml` and `delete-stale-branches.yml` now fire on a **commit** (`.github/release/VERSION`, `.github/stale-branches.txt`), the same push-trigger route `post-discussion.yml` already used; Supabase edge deploys and SQL migrations go through the **Management API with `$SUPABASE_PAT`** when the MCP tools are blocked. The stale-branch list was **55 entries of which 54 no longer existed**; the remote is now `main` + `gh-pages` only.
- **2026-08-19 (v10.202.0: Fundamentals — the Indian Wheel of Power & Powerlessness; PR #935, branch `claude/fundamentals-clickable-map-03r24i`)**: User asked for a "Fundamentals" page exploring the wheel as a **clickable map with data justifying the placement of groups**, and to "give due credit". Built `/fundamentals.html` — 12 axes x 3 rings = 36 SVG segments, each opening a panel of sourced Indian evidence. Files: `js/fundamentals-data.js` (dataset), `js/fundamentals-wheel.js` (render + interaction), `css/fundamentals.css`. **The wheel is NOT ours** — The Listeners Collective, Bengaluru (Disha Paul, Ayush Biswas, Trupti M., Ganga Nair; developed 2022, published 2023), adapting Sylvia Duckworth's Wheel of Power/Privilege made for the Canadian Council for Refugees; credit is a full section on the page + footer + both changelogs, with the lineage (Crenshaw, Collins; Ambedkar, the Phules, Chakravarti, Rege, Guru). **What we added: 113 evidence entries**, each with a named source + year, web-verified before writing (NFHS-5, PLFS 2023-24, Census 2011, NSS 76th Round, NCRB 2022, AISHE 2021-22, ASER 2024, Time Use Survey 2019, NITI Aayog National MPI, World Inequality Lab 2024, UNFPA/IIPS Ageing 2023, Access Now 2023, UNESCO, Sachar 2006, IHDS-II). **Editorial rule: nothing unsourced admitted** — the gaps are shown, not filled (sexual orientation uncounted in every national survey; disability 2.2% vs global ~16%; no Indian survey collects skin tone/appearance; no caste census beyond SC/ST since 1931). Per-axis "what this does not settle" note; optional "mark where you sit" (localStorage) deliberately with **no score and no total**. Wiring: index.html Showcase card (backup taken), search-index `fundamentals` (type `showcase`), sitemap.xml +1, sitemap.html +1 (also fixed a pre-existing off-by-one in its "Explore & Community" count), docs/changelog.md For Learners + CHANGELOG.md. All guards PASS; 16 CI checks green. **LIVE at impactmojo.in/fundamentals.html** (PR #935 merged, v10.202.0; deploy verified). Follow-ups the same session: **#936** finished the language pass on copy outside the page body (homepage card, meta/OG/Twitter, search-index). **#937** (v10.203.0) fixed discoverability — it had shipped linked only from one homepage card 41% down the page (17th of 18 in a scroll strip) and sitemap.html; now in the `site-chrome.js` footer 'Explore' column (sitewide) and in `libraries.html` under Learn & Practice, which was also stale for **The Long View** and **Marginalia** (both added; Skills Profiler deliberately left out — the page's lede scopes it to reference *collections*, and the profiler is a single tool). #937 also fixed `libraries.html` JSON-LD drift (numberOfItems 12 vs 13 listed → 16/16). **Owner directive that drove #936: "this has so much AI language"** — the page copy read as machine-generated. Tells found and removed: 99 em-dashes in the data file (→23); every evidence caption on the same beat (fact — dash — editorial kicker); the "X is not Y, it is Z" opener on most axes; an epigram closing nearly every paragraph; a literal "It is not a score / It is not additive / It is not fixed" triad; self-congratulation ("an honest note", "the disagreements are the point"). Rewrote 36 editorial strings + 55 captions + hero/method/credits + renderer copy; **no figure, source, year or placement changed** (113 entries re-counted across all 36 bands after). **Apply this standard to future user-facing copy.**

- **2026-07-27 (v10.152.0: ASER + NFHS explorer parity + context; PR #856)**: The two Studio data explorers (`/aser.html`, `/nfhs.html`) looked like unrelated products — unified them. Both now share the standard **site-chrome.js** (top bar/footer/WhatsApp FAB/global nav), the **"ImpactMojo Studio · <topic>"** eyebrow, **Amaranth** headline, **stats strip**, **"How to read" box**, and the fill-in-the-blanks question composer. ASER additionally gained a plain-language *"What is ASER — and why it matters"* primer and a *"Making sense of the numbers"* methodology section (what a "pass" means, how to read the 2022 pandemic dip and the private-vs-government gap responsibly, district-sample caveats, full sources). **KEY BUG FIXED (was live):** `site-chrome.js`'s kill-list strips `.foot`/`.footer`/`footer`/`.site-footer` and any body>header/nav — ASER's methodology used `class="foot"` so it was **invisible on the deployed page**; renamed to `class="method"`. Same trap on NFHS: sources lived in `<footer class="nf-foot">` (stripped) and JS wrote to `#nf-src` inside it (would have thrown) — moved to `<section class="nf-method">`; its custom `nf-top` header is now replaced by the standard bar. **LESSON for any site-chrome page: never put content in `.foot/.footer/footer/.site-footer` or a body-level `<header>/<nav>` — it gets removed at runtime.** Verified via headless render (matching chrome, no double header, map intact) + live curl markers. All 15 CI checks green.
- **2026-07-27 (v10.151.0: ASER Explorer district drill-down; PR #854/#855)**: Added a "Zoom into the districts" view — pick a state, rank its surveyed districts for 2024 by reading/arithmetic, following ASER's own Std III–V and VI–VIII bands with the state average marked and small-sample districts named. **605 districts across 27 states**, compiled from ASER 2024's per-state "Performance of districts" PDF estimates (asercentre.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/12/<State>_District-Estimates.pdf; HP=POS_HP.pdf, Punjab=POS_Punjab.pdf). Parsed by **column geometry** (PyMuPDF `fitz`; names = alpha tokens LEFT of first numeric column to drop right-side "Data insufficient" footnotes; state-total row = column template). Validated: every state-total round-trips within districts' min/max, all values in [0,100], Bihar=exactly 38 districts, zero districts-with-data lost. Parser + data preserved in scratchpad `aser/distest/` (parse_de.py, district_parsed.json, aser_dist.js). District data is 2024-only (historical per-round district data not published consistently).

- **2026-07-12 (Fix: stale service-worker cache — "can't see any updates"; branch `claude/book-companions-j7wixj`, v10.111.0)**: User: "I can't see any of the earlier updates... homepage edits are exactly the same... bookmarks etc have not even changed." **DIAGNOSIS via curl of LIVE site** (all changes WERE deployed): homepage shows "31 labs" live; `/BookSummaries/`→301→`/booksummaries/` has `#booksSearch`+`load-more-btn`+`count">128`; `/Labs/before-we-fall-apart-lab.html`→301→`/labs/before-we-fall-apart-lab` 200. So Netlify deploys fine — the user saw stale content from the **PWA service worker**. ROOT: `service-worker.js` `const VERSION='v2-2026-07-06'` — **not bumped in 6 days** despite many deploys, though the SW's own header comment says "Bump VERSION on a deploy that changes cached static assets." SW design (confirmed): install→`skipWaiting()`; activate→delete all caches except current `im-runtime-'+VERSION` and `impactmojo-course-*`→`clients.claim()`; HTML=networkFirst, assets(css/js/img/json + gstatic/jsdelivr CDN)=staleWhileRevalidate. Because the SW file was byte-identical, browsers never detected an update → old `im-runtime-v2-2026-07-06` cache persisted → SWR served old JS/CSS. (HTML is networkFirst so mostly fresh; homepage HTTP cache-control is only max-age=300, SW is max-age=0 must-revalidate — both fine.) **FIX**: bumped `VERSION` to `v2-2026-07-12` → new SW installs, purges old cache, claims clients → users get fresh assets on next visit. Ran `node scripts/test-service-worker.mjs` → PASS (7 groups; required by CI). changelog v10.111.0. Told user how to force-refresh now (DevTools→Application→Service Workers→Unregister, or hard reload). **AUTO-FIX (same PR #765, after user asked "didn't we have auto version check + bump?")**: there WAS a half-mechanism — `netlify.toml` `[build] command` wrote `version.json` (a UTC timestamp) each deploy, and `js/auto-refresh.js` polls it every 60s and `window.location.reload()`s the page on change. BUT a page reload does NOT purge the SW's stale-while-revalidate asset cache — only a SW `VERSION` change does — and nothing auto-stamped the SW VERSION. So added `scripts/stamp-version.sh` (chmod +x) that stamps BOTH version.json AND `service-worker.js`'s `const VERSION` with the deploy id (`${COMMIT_REF:0:12}` on Netlify, else UTC timestamp) via `sed`; changed netlify.toml `command` to `bash scripts/stamp-version.sh`. Runs on Netlify's ephemeral checkout so it never commits back — committed SW VERSION stays a clean default (`v2-2026-07-12`), Netlify overrides per-deploy. Tested both modes locally + `node scripts/test-service-worker.mjs` PASS with a stamped version; reverted local mutations after test so repo stays clean.
- **2026-07-12 (Fix: homepage tour hung at "101 deck"; branch `claude/book-companions-j7wixj`, v10.110.0)**: User: "homepage tour pauses at 101 deck, hangs and then exits." Tour = Intro.js via `js/tours.js` + `css/tour-theme.css`; index tour steps include `#nav-courses` = the **"101 Course Decks"** nav item, which lives INSIDE the Learn dropdown (`#learn-dropdown.dropdown-menu.specials-grouped` under `li#nav-learn.has-dropdown`). **ROOT CAUSE**: the nav collapses dropdowns via `max-height:0; overflow:hidden` and reveals them by adding `.open` (→ `max-height:500px`, and for specials-grouped `max-height:none`). tours.js instead added its OWN class `.tour-dropdown-open`, whose CSS (tour-theme.css) set only `display/visibility/opacity` — NEVER released `max-height`. So the menu stayed clipped to 0 height; Intro.js highlighted a hidden zero-height `#nav-courses` and hung, then exited. (`getBoundingClientRect` returns layout height ignoring ancestor overflow-clip, which is why it wasn't obviously "missing".) **FIX (2 parts)**: (1) `js/tours.js` now adds the nav's real `.open` class alongside `.tour-dropdown-open` in all 3 spots (`ensureDropdownVisible`, `onbeforechange`, and removes both in `closeAllTourDropdowns`) — reuses the tested reveal incl. specials-grouped grid; (2) `css/tour-theme.css` `.tour-dropdown-open > .dropdown-menu` now also sets `max-height:none !important; overflow:visible !important` as a belt-and-suspenders fallback. **VERIFIED headless** (playwright-core + chromium_headless_shell; local `python3 -m http.server 8199` so `/css/*` absolute paths resolve; injected intro.js 8.3.2 fetched via `curl` since the cdnjs CDN is proxy-blocked in-sandbox): drove the real tour via `.introjs-nextbutton` JS-clicks — it now runs all 8 surviving steps to Done, and at the `#nav-courses` step `menuClipped:false` + element height 47px (previously would hang). NOTE: `#nav-games` & `#nav-dataverse` tour steps target non-existent IDs and are silently filtered out by the existing `filtered` logic — harmless, left as-is. changelog v10.110.0.
- **2026-07-12 (Reading Companions landing — search + pagination; branch `claude/book-companions-j7wixj`, v10.109.0)**: User: "this book summaries is also becoming one long list of cards. can we paginate or otherwise make the landing better?" With 128 cards in a flat grid (`BookSummaries/index.html`), no search, and a lopsided category filter (catch-all "policy" = 52). Added (single-file, no new deps): instant **search box** (`#booksSearch` — filters on cached `card.dataset.search` = lowercased textContent), **"Load more" pagination** (`PAGE_SIZE=24`, `#loadMoreBtn`), **live count** (`#booksCount` "Showing N of 128"), **no-results state** (`#noResults` + Clear-filters `#clearSearch`) — all unified with the existing category `.filter-btn`s via one `apply()`. Rewrote the old show/hide-only filter JS. Added matching CSS (search pill, count, load-more btn, link-btn). **Verified headless** (playwright-core + `/opt/pw-browsers/chromium_headless_shell-1194`, file:// + `ctx.route('**')` abort external): total 128 → init 24 visible → count text correct → Load more → 48 → search "savarkar" → 2 correct hits → nonsense → 0 + no-results shown → clear → 24 → data-viz filter → 17, **zero pageerrors**. Did NOT overhaul the imbalanced category taxonomy (would need re-tagging all 128 cards) — search compensates; flagged as possible follow-up. changelog v10.109.0.

That resource is a facilitated 12-month transformative-justice cohort program (not a downloadable artifact) — so we can't clone it, but built its self-contained interactive counterpart. Via AskUserQuestion user chose "Interactive studio Lab (capstone)". **Built** `Labs/before-we-fall-apart-lab.html` — a group conflict-preparedness studio (6 tabs: Why prepare · Shared agreements · Tripwires & needs · Rupture→repair protocol · Accountability pods · Your charter). Browser-local only (localStorage key `bwfa-studio`), exports a "relational charter" as text + print; respectful signpost to Alternative Justice's actual programme (new tab). Chrome copied from sibling `Labs/nvc-mediation-lab.html` (warm restorative accent #B45309/#0EA5E9). **GOTCHA**: spawned a general-purpose subagent to build it; its `.output` sat at 121 bytes frozen for ~6 min so I assumed the documented "backgrounded work" stall and started hand-writing the file — but the agent actually completed at ~7.5 min (big single-file builds only flush at the end; my Write correctly refused to clobber the agent's on-disk file). LESSON: a frozen small `.output` is NOT proof of a stall for a long single-Write task — wait for the notification. **FIX after review**: the agent removed the supabase CDN + auth.js to honour a literal "no CDN" instruction, but retained `/js/state-manager.js`, `/js/translate-sarvam.js`, `/js/config.js` which reference the `Supabase` global → would throw. Restored the sibling's EXACT script block (supabase CDN + auth.js) for parity; studio data still never leaves the browser (supabase is only topbar auth). **WIRING**: Lab count 30→31 (Labs/index.html title/og/hero/section-title + meta descs, index.html, catalog.html, README.md, docs/platform-overview.md ×2); +1 card (data-track communication); **corrected pre-existing Labs filter-chip drift** (chips summed 28 but 30 cards — policy 4→5, communication 1→3, All →31); +search-index entry (id before-we-fall-apart-lab); +sitemap; +catalog entry (id l31, max was l30); flagship `courses/nvc-rj/index.html` gets a hero-resource-btn + a Practice-section capstone card. changelog v10.108.0. - **2026-07-12 (22 new Reading Companions — South Asian politics/history/society; branch `claude/book-companions-j7wixj`, PR #761, v10.107.0)**: User "I added several new books to the Google folder" (Drive `1nhfRPcbEvXxXiRDXG1hVWQZbm2VdJazL`). Diffed createdTime → **22 genuinely new files**. Built all 22, Reading Companions **106→128**. **PIPELINE**: one general-purpose subagent per book following `scratchpad/SPEC_GUIDE.md` FULL-TEXT METHOD — `curl -sL "https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id=<ID>"` (bypasses MCP 10MB cap) → `scripts/extract-pdf-text.py` (PyMuPDF + Tesseract) → write `specs2/<slug>.json`; parent runs `scripts/build-book-companion.py`. Books: shadows-at-noon, muslim-zion, jinnealogy, savarkar-chaturvedi (Hindutva & Violence), islam-in-south-asia, prophets-facing-backward, new-populism, rules-for-radicals, majoritarianism-in-india, women-in-india, empires-backyard, sea-subalternity-sundarbans, meat-eating-in-india, anti-muslim-violence-india, bulldozer-justice, secularism-adcock, ajaz-ashraf (Bhima Koregaon), indian-political-thought (Kapila Violent Fraternity), fascism-in-india, panthers-in-parliament, audrey-truschke-india, queer-digital-india. 4 are journal articles — structured by real article sections, labelled honestly. **GOTCHA — subagents backgrounding OCR**: some agents ended their turn with OCR still running in a bg shell → no spec written. Fix: relaunch pointed at the already-extracted local `/tmp/<slug>.txt`, run extraction FOREGROUND, verify spec written before returning. **WIRING** (idempotent `scratchpad/wire_new_books.py`): +22 cards, +22 search-index BS107–BS128, +22 catalog bs107–bs128, +22 sitemap; count 106→128 in premium/README/platform-overview/content-catalog + bn/ta/hi/mr + faq.html + content-marketing-kit.html + freemium-and-premium-guide.md (several pre-drifted to 105 on main — bumped all to 128; historical changelog/CHANGELOG/memory LEFT). **BUG**: generator pre-inserted `&mdash;` before esc_html → `&amp;mdash;`; fixed via sed. **MERGE GOTCHA**: main advanced ~61 commits (to v10.106.0) during the multi-hour build → PR #761 hit conflicts on append-heavy files. Resolved by `git checkout -B <branch> origin/main`, restoring the 22 companion HTMLs from a scratchpad stash, re-running the idempotent wiring script on fresh main, renumbering changelog →v10.107.0, force-push, re-merge. LESSON: append-only wiring script + companion-file stash makes rebase-onto-fresh-main trivial. **Also survived a mid-flow container restart** (working tree persisted; only the memory edit needed re-applying). Guards: check-book-companions PASS, search-index JSON valid, mojibake PASS. No duplicates. **FINAL: 128 Reading Companions live.**

Verified all 12 URLs 200 live.
- **2026-06-05 (cont. — site-wide Sarvam language switcher, PR #551)**: Pivoted from static per-page translated copies to ONE on-demand runtime translation layer (user: "language switcher with sarvam... for the whole site incl. courses"). (1) **`netlify/functions/translate.mjs`** (`/api/translate`): Sarvam **Mayura** proxy with **Netlify Blobs** cache — each unique (string,lang) translated once then served from cache; key server-side only; bounded concurrency + 429/5xx backoff; **dynamic `@netlify/blobs` import** so it degrades to live-translate if Blobs missing. (2) **`js/translate-sarvam.js`**: floating EN/हिन्दी/தமিழ்/বাংলা/मराठी switcher on **all 377 user-facing pages**; walks text nodes, batches misses (60) to the function, localStorage cache, swaps in Noto script font (Devanagari hi/mr, Tamil ta, Bengali bn), restores English instantly; **MutationObserver** translates deferred/dynamic content (flagship Supabase module bodies, auth bar); **brand-safe** KEEP list (ImpactMojo etc. never translated). (3) **KEY GOTCHA: there are 25 Netlify sites on acct `ilw`; the production site is `impactmojo` id `f9e879bf-4792-42aa-96ea-c3a3b396f8b8` (www.impactmojo.in)** — a `?name=impactmojo` lookup wrongly matched `im-industrygroups` (industrygroups.impactmojo.in) first. Set `SARVAM_API_KEY` env (functions scope) on the CORRECT site. (4) **Env vars only apply to deploys built AFTER they're set** → had to push an empty commit to rebuild the preview before the function saw the key (was returning 503). (5) VERIFIED on deploy-preview-551: all 4 languages translate (function 200s + correct script + Noto fonts + no overflow + brand preserved). (6) Fixed axe SERIOUS color-contrast on switcher buttons (grey 4.48:1 → #475569 7.6:1). **COST MODEL: bounded = unique strings × 4 langs, one-time warm-up then cached (Blobs + localStorage), NOT per-pageview.** **COLD-CACHE UX: first translate of a heavy page ~30-45s (909 strings on a deck), instant after — added "translating…" indicator; pre-warming top pages is the mitigation.** **SARVAM_API_KEY**: user pasted in chat → stored gitignored `.claude/.env.keys` + Netlify env; ADVISE ROTATION. Playwright+Chromium installed in env (pip) for headless verification; deploy-preview subdomains need `ignore_https_errors=True`. **TODO**: merge #551 on green; pre-warm top ~20 pages × 4; remove redundant static `101-courses/hi/data-lit.html`; consider role/landmark for switcher (non-blocking region warning).

Added YAML frontmatter so the skill description is properly indexed by Claude Code.
  - **Made housekeeping skill globally available** by also installing it at `~/.claude/skills/housekeeping/SKILL.md` (now invokable from any session, any repo on this machine, with a fallback path). Both copies kept in sync.
  
  **Key learnings captured in the new skill:**
  - Always start with a tight allowlist for find-and-replace, not a broad denylist (the duplicate-topbar over-correction taught this — `.v3-organic-bg`, `.reading-progress`, `<th>`, `.result-flash` are NOT navigation)
  - Test on ONE file first, verify diff, THEN run across the repo
  - Compare imbalance against HEAD (delta=0), not absolute, since some files have pre-existing imbalances
  - Skip emoji/string matches inside `<!-- comments -->`, `<script>`, `<style>`, `<svg>`, and `Backups/`
  - The canonical theme localStorage key is `im-theme` — never introduce a new one
  - Handout pages (84) and main site pages use SEPARATE topbar templates and that's correct; never auto-add `im-topbar` to a page that already has a real `<header>` main nav

- **2026-04-07 (continued)**: Workflow correctness + axe baseline clear marathon. Shipped PR #358 (housekeeping batch, replacing earlier #357) and PR #359 (8-pass a11y baseline clear).

  **#358 (housekeeping):**
  - 213 files: 61 count drift fixes across 28 files, CHANGELOG v10.14.0, ROADMAP Q2 update, 100% Google Analytics coverage on 185 pages (89 added), 100% SEO baseline on 185 pages (1,425 meta tags inserted), `Backups/index-backup-20260407-1001-after-brand-cleanup.html`, `.claude/memory.md` Session Log + 5 Recent Decisions
  - **`.claude/skills/housekeeping/SKILL.md` rewritten 169 → 922 lines** with YAML frontmatter, count-drift script, false-positive avoidance, brand identity audit, duplicate-header detection allowlist, WCAG token canon, emoji→Sargam mapping, Parcel-bundle handling, enliven-repo workflow, content-type checklists. Also installed at `~/.claude/skills/housekeeping/SKILL.md` for global availability.
  - Catalog.html aria-hidden-focus fix (emoji→Sargam script had inserted `<img>` inside `placeholder=""` attribute on `#searchInput`)

  **#359 (workflow + a11y baseline):**
  - **CI workflow correctness fix** — both `.github/workflows/accessibility.yml` and `.github/workflows/ci.yml` had `$?` capture after `npm run test:a11y | tee` which always returned 0 (tee's exit code), so the wrapper script always took the success branch. PRs #350–#358 all silently passed despite real a11y violations. Fixed with `${PIPESTATUS[0]}`. Also discovered `tee` + `2>&1` was truncating pa11y's JSON output to ~17% of full size (65KB vs 393KB) — switched to direct stdout redirection (`pa11y-ci ... > pa11y-results.json`) which avoids the pipe entirely. Pa11y JSON parser also bumped to use depth-counted brace walking with diagnostic catch logging.
  - **Tests config**: Added third-party widget exclusions to `tests/axe-accessibility.js` — `.introjs-*` (auto-starts via `js/tours.js` 1.5s delay), `.userway_*`, `#uwy*`, `.gtranslate_wrapper`. These are vendor-injected DOM that we don't directly own.
  - **8-pass axe baseline clear**: 11 → 6 → 5 → 3 → 2 → 1 → 1 → 1 → **0**. Fixes spanned `index.html` (.imx-tag-muted token bump, 10 inline Explore Course CTA colors, 8 flagship feature spans, .imx-resource-cta, .v3-hero-eyebrow, .imx-track-flagship-pill, community card badges + CTAs, premium tier headers + descriptions, footer link rules, footer compliance opacity), `about.html` (.lang-status both variants, .team-role, .upi-box h4, global a + footer), `catalog.html` (.card-rating, .card-track, 5 .card-type variants), `bct-repository.html` (14 text uses of accent-color via override block, .sector-pill, .technique-id-badge fixed dark bg, .compare-bar-label, .footer-bottom a, --gradient-primary token), `courses/mel/index.html` (global a + link-in-text-block rule), and `js/auth.js` (Sign Up button background #6366f1 → #4f46e5, fallback #94a3b8 → #475569 across Login/Account/Logout).
  - **Pa11y trajectory** (cascade from axe fixes): 720 → 577 → ~414 → ~410 (plateaued — remaining errors are on the 8 pages outside axe's set).
  - **Issue #360** opened to track pa11y baseline cleanup as a clean follow-up (lexicons, handouts, premium, dataverse, etc.). Strategy documented: run pa11y locally, identify top 3-5 token clusters, apply scoped overrides, target 0-20 errors.

  **Final session totals:**
  - 9 PRs merged to main (#350–#356, #358, #359) + 1 closed (#357 superseded)
  - 1 follow-up issue opened (#360)
  - Skill globally available + 922-line checklist locked in
  - axe-core: 0 serious violations on all 5 audited pages (was hiding 11)
  - pa11y: 720 → 410 errors (still red, tracked in #360)
  - Google Analytics: 100% coverage on 185 content pages
  - SEO baseline: 100% coverage on 185 content pages
  - Content counts unified across 28 files
  - CI gates now truthful — any new a11y regression will be caught and reported correctly

  **Key reusable learnings (added to housekeeping skill):**
  - `tee` + `2>&1` mixing stderr into stdout can corrupt large JSON output. Use direct redirection (`> file.json`) for capturing data; let stderr go to the workflow log.
  - `$?` after a pipe captures the LAST command's exit code. Use `${PIPESTATUS[0]}` for the upstream command.
  - Brand color text on light brand-tinted backgrounds is the #1 a11y anti-pattern. Always use a darker variant for text (sky-500 → sky-800, indigo-500 → indigo-700, etc.).
  - `opacity: 0.8` on text using `var(--text-secondary)` puts contrast right at the 4.5:1 threshold. Either remove the opacity or use a darker base color.
  - JS-injected DOM (auth widgets, tours, sticky bots) needs its own a11y audit — fallback colors in `style.cssText` strings often use `#94a3b8` which fails AA.
  - Inline `style="..."` overrides cascade rules unless `!important`. Bulk fixing inline color requires sed or per-element edits.
  - Third-party widget DOM (Intro.js, UserWay, Google Translate) should be excluded from axe via `axe.run({exclude: [...]})` rather than fixing the vendor's HTML.
  - The pa11y JSON parser needs depth-counted brace walking (not `lastIndexOf('}')`) because JSON strings can contain `}` literals.

- **APPROACH (key technique)**: surgical transplant, not a hand-rewrite. Embedded devecon's ENTIRE `<head>`+CSS verbatim (the shared design system) + devecon body chrome (im-topbar, V3 decorations, reading-progress, mobile-header, `.sidebar` w/ sectioned `nav-link`s, `.main-content>.content-wrapper`, footer, all chrome JS) + devecon hero pattern, then KEPT the Power BI body content (8 modules, interface mockups, 22 sketches, calculators, 40 quiz `.q`s, DAX, callouts, capstone, data sources, connected, founders) **byte-for-byte**. Built by a Python assembler `/tmp/assemble_pbi.py` slicing devecon lines [1:3089]=head+css, [3623:3663]=footer, [3665:3949]=chrome JS+GA+UserWay, [3988:4064]=coach+im-theme JS; powerbi lines [412:942]=content.
- **THE TRICK that made it cheap & safe**: a small alias `<style>` block maps Power BI's local tokens to the shared ones — `--ink→var(--text-primary)`, `--card→var(--card-bg)`, `--line→var(--border-color)`, `--display/--head→var(--font-serif)`, `--soft→var(--hover-bg)`, etc. So every PBI component inherited flagship typography/colour/dark-mode automatically, with NO edits to the ported component CSS. Component CSS (modules/callouts/labs/pbi-mockups/quiz/objectives/etc.) kept verbatim; only DROPPED PBI's global resets + `.top`/`.shell`/`.side`/`.hero`/`footer`/global `table` (scoped tables to `.tbl-wrap`). Kalam re-added via `@import` for the `.hand` sketch labels.
- Gotchas handled: (1) devecon's inline `.hero-tagline` is `color:rgba(255,255,255,0.9)` = invisible in light mode (latent devecon bug, only OK in dark because devecon hero has NO bg) → set PBI tagline to `var(--accent-color)`. (2) devecon `.section`/`.reveal` are NOT opacity:0 (reveal defaults to opacity:1) so PBI sections w/o `.reveal` stay visible. (3) DROPPED `course-loader.js`/`course-progress.js` from the deferred-scripts list (those drive devecon's module-PLACEHOLDER system; PBI content is inline). (4) kept PBI's quiz JS (`.q`/`.opt`), used devecon's scrollspy/active-nav instead of PBI's.
- **Fixed a pre-existing broken link**: page linked `lexicon.html` but the file is `powerbilexicon.html` (the `_redirects` rule only covers extensionless `/courses/powerbi/lexicon`). Changed both hrefs → `powerbilexicon.html`. NOTE: `powerbilexicon.html` ITSELF still has the same broken back-link AND is still on the OLD bespoke design — a future task is to re-skin the lexicon page the same way.

### Session 2026-06-05 — 101 Series Gamma→native migration COMPLETE (26 decks) + deck-builder pipeline (v10.54.0–10.59.0)
- **Task**: user wanted ALL 101 courses moved off embedded Gamma iframes to native HTML, "beautifully formatted, charts/diagrams, no extra whitespace". Mid-task clarification: **each course must be EXACTLY 100 slides**.
- **Landscape**: `101-courses/` had 7 pre-existing native decks (big self-contained ~300-400KB HTML: dev-economics, mel-basics, climate-essentials, social-margins, public-finance-budgeting, work-labour-livelihoods, inequality-basics) + 26 Gamma stubs (~4.4KB iframe embeds of `gamma.app/embed/<id>`). The `native/` subdir (shared deck.css/deck.js) was an abandoned approach — only a 3-slide stub. Gold standard = the big SELF-CONTAINED decks (inline CSS+JS), `dev-economics.html` is the canonical donor.
- **Pipeline built — `scripts/deck-builder/`** (the key leverage): `build.py` slices the proven shell (head <style> ~430 lines + closing runtime JS: theme switcher, viewport scaling, lazy chart init, deep-linking, auto-fit/compact modes, ECharts resize) OUT of dev-economics.html via markers (`<div class="slide-viewport" id="viewport">` … `</div><!-- /slide-viewport -->`) and injects generated slides + SLIDE_IDS + the Chart.js `initChart` body + ECharts body, swapping SEO/title strings. So every deck inherits identical polish for free. Each deck = a compact Python spec in `specs/<slug>.py` exporting `DECK={slug,title,description,slides:[...]}`. Component block types (t=): body,label,title,bullets,stats,twocol,panel,hbox,term,quote,table,flow,darkcard,chart,raw. Slide types: title,toc,divider,content,end. Charts: Chart.js; `data`/`options` are Python literals (json.dumps'd) — raw JS via `{"__js__":"..."}` wrapper. Schema doc: `specs/_schema.md`.
- **100-slide HARD RULE**: build refuses to write (and `--check` exits non-zero) unless exactly 100 navigable slides. Builder AUTO-COMPUTES TOC "Slides X–Y" ranges from divider positions (authors give TOC items `name` only) so they never drift. = title + toc + 11 dividers + 86 content + end.
- **Production**: authored Data Literacy myself as the gold-standard benchmark, then delegated the rest to general-purpose subagents (1 spec each, detailed prompt + accuracy guardrails, they run `--check` until PASS). My pipeline per deck: build → validate (div/script balance, no `: True/False/None` Python-literal leaks in chart JS, every canvas has an `mk()` init call, no dev-economics leftovers, 100 ids) → accuracy spot-check (grep key claims) → `rebadge.py` → commit. Clusters: Data&Methods (10), Gender&Social (6), Critical&Digital (5), Governance&Economy (5: ind-constitution, dev-architecture, pub-health-basics, fundraising-basics, eng-dev). RESULT: 33 native / 0 Gamma / 7 coming.
- **Tooling**: `scripts/deck-builder/rebadge.py` re-badges a card (card-badge-deck→native) AND recomputes index counts. **BUG fixed**: first version counted bare `card-badge-native` which ALSO matches the CSS rules + JS classify() → inflated counts; fixed to count the full span token `class="card-badge card-badge-native"`. `scripts/deck-builder/sync_search_index.py` SPLICES (not re-dumps — avoids reflowing 595 entries) one `course` entry per migrated deck into `data/search-index.json` (+26 → 621, the convention is native 101 decks get indexed; the 26 Gamma ones never were).
- **GOTCHAS**: (1) A context/session transition (session id changed 2928c909→cb230d43) ORPHANED the first Governance agent batch — they never delivered notifications; re-launched fresh (idempotent) and they completed normally (~8 min real time). (2) The stop-hook fires on every turn-end while bg agents run; turns are SECONDS apart so it FEELS like agents are stalled when really little wall-clock has passed — be patient, don't manually redo. (3) Building a deck from an intermediate `--check` poll BEFORE the agent's completion notification can be STALE — the post_truth agent kept polishing (4→5 charts) after I'd built/committed; always rebuild from the final spec after the completion notification (I added a rebuild-and-diff safety pass). (4) sitemap.xml already had all 26 URLs (migration reuses URLs) — nothing to add there. (5) Title slide shows "01" in its footer (donor omits it) — harmless, left as-is.
- STATE: migration done. To add a NEW 101 deck: write `specs/<slug>.py` (100 slides), `python3 scripts/deck-builder/build.py <module>`, `rebadge.py <slug>`, `sync_search_index.py`, commit. Index "Slide Deck" stat/filter removed (now 0); hero shows "3,300 Slides of Learning".

GATES that made it safe: `node --check` every remaining inline script + **zero-residual-reference scan** (no inline code may still call applyTheme/getPreferredTheme/updateButtons/getSystemTheme) + Playwright behaviour check (toggle still changes bg, no theme console errors). ### Session 2026-06-05 (c) — Mojini FAQ-bank fix (#564, LIVE) + permanent mojibake guard
- **Live-site verification** (user asked to confirm impactmojo.in works): all key pages HTTP 200; this session's deploys (js/theme.js, css/cookie.css, imx-main z-index scale) live; theme toggle works live desktop+mobile. Surfaced one pre-existing runtime error → chased it down.
- **Real bug fixed (#564, merged + deployed)**: `js/faq-bank.js:191` had a mojibake'd em-dash rendered as two ASCII double-quotes INSIDE a double-quoted string (`...course or lab"" Mojini...`), closing the string early → `Uncaught SyntaxError: Unexpected string`. That parse error killed the ENTIRE faq-bank.js IIFE, so the Mojini chatbot's FAQ matching + course/lab listing never loaded (broken since #488, 2026-06-03). Fixed the em-dash + cleaned 2 bullets (`"¢ `→`• `) and 29 em-dashes (` "" ${`→` — ${`) in the (valid, template-literal) display strings. Verified: faqBankV1 installs, 34 courses exposed.
  - RED HERRING: first suspected `index.html:6431` `['What's Premium?'...]` apostrophe — but that whole Mojini KB-enhancer `<script>` is inside an unclosed HTML comment (6382–6538), so it never executes. `node --check` flags it (regex ignores HTML comments); the HTML-parser-based check correctly found 0 broken EXECUTING scripts. Don't trust regex-extracted `<script>` node-checks across HTML comments.
- **Mojibake sweep + PERMANENT guard** (user: "sweep + permanent fix/protection"): scanned 918 text files — the historical corruption is essentially GONE (classic `Ã…`/`â€…`/`Â…`/U+FFFD/C1-control families all clean). Only remnants: faq-bank.js (above) + 4 spots in index.html's DEAD commented Mojini block (cleaned: 3 `""`→`—`, 1 `founders""¦`→`…`).
  - **`scripts/check-mojibake.py`** — high-signal, ZERO-false-positive detector (detection only, no auto-fix). Patterns use \u escapes (pure-ASCII source). Skips its own file (its regexes contain the hunted chars). Catches Ã©/â€™/"¢/""¦; flags NONE of legit Unicode (— … ₹ © § · ± smart quotes, Devanagari/Tamil/Bengali/Marathi i18n). Limitation: bare ASCII `""`-as-em-dash is undetectable (indistinguishable from empty strings).
  - **CI**: wired as the `encoding` job in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` → fails the build on any reintroduced signature. Documented as checklist item 6 in `.claude/rules/testing.md`. Added `__pycache__/` to `.gitignore`.
  - LEFT ALONE (flagged for user): `scripts/gender-pubpol-seed.json` has a lone `10¢` in a stat-card — ambiguous intent, it's a seed script (content already in Supabase), and a bare `¢` isn't a guard signature (false-positive risk).
- GOTCHA: don't put LITERAL mojibake examples in shipped docs/checker prose — the guard scans .md and will flag them; describe corruption by name instead. RESOLVED for internal notes: the guard now skips `.claude/` (SKIP_DIRS) — it protects *shipped* site content only, so memory.md/rules/skills may carry literal examples freely. Live-site Playwright needs `ignore_https_errors=True` + `--ignore-certificate-errors` (sandbox TLS proxy); `window.onerror` (filename/lineno) pinpoints parse errors that pageerror reports with no location.
- **SHIPPED to main (2026-06-05 end-of-session)**: mojibake guard merged via **PR #565** (CI `encoding` job now live on main — future PRs reintroducing a signature fail the build). Marginalia intro rewrite merged via **PR #566**: readers found the original hard to follow (metaphors needing decoding — "someone in a lanyard," a doubled margin/script/filed image). Offered 3 rewrites; user picked **"clear but keeps wit"** — lede+body of `specials/marginalia/index.html` now plainly state the device (the sector's claim vs. the field's reply + a real footnote). Meta/OG/twitter descriptions were already clear, left unchanged. No changelog/search-index/sitemap edits (existing page; sitemap already had the entry; copy-only clarification, no new content/counts).
