### Session 2026-06-11 (b) — Live Case Challenge packets (v10.79.0, PR #625)
- **Trigger**: follow-on from the v10.78.0 session. The 9 Live Case Challenges (`data/challenges.json`) had `resources` arrays that were ALL `url:"#"` placeholders (the "kuch resources open nahi" bug) — js fix shipped "coming soon", but the real downloadable case files never existed. User asked to build one as a quality sample, approved it, then said "merge & build all 8 more".
- **What shipped**: real case packets under `/challenges/<id>/` for all 9 challenges — 24 brand-styled `noindex` HTML docs + 6 CSV datasets. Built mel-logframe-redesign myself as the gold standard (program-description / flawed-logframe / baseline-data + CSV), then delegated the other 8 to parallel general-purpose subagents (1 challenge each), each told to READ `data/challenges.json` for its `caseContext` + the sample packet for the brand, produce documents matching the resource labels, and REPORT a `resources` array (NOT edit challenges.json). I wired all 8 arrays centrally in one pass.
- **Quality bar**: artifacts are internally consistent with each case — the flawed logframe contains its 5 documented flaws (activity-as-impact indicator, disconnected outcomes, empty assumptions, no learning questions, 4/7 missing MoV) un-annotated; the devai dataset exhibits the 4 described biases and is detectable in cross-tabs; the two nutrition interventions' cost breakdowns reconcile to ₹8,500 / ₹1,200 per child-month and outcomes to 68%/41% recovery; RTI doc has verified s.2–s.20 section numbers; Gandhi excerpts are genuine public-domain, attributed; SEL uses CASEL-5 attributed.
- **Brand template for a case-packet doc**: head (charset/viewport/title/meta-desc/`robots noindex,follow`/canonical/GA G-JRCMEB9TBW/Amaranth+Inter+JetBrains fonts), `:root` light/dark vars, `.topbar` back-link to `/challenges.html`, `.doc-tag` "Case Packet · Document N of M", composite-case + CC BY-NC-ND footer, closing one-line theme `<script>` reading `im-theme`. Datasets = real CSV fronted by an HTML view with a download button (the resource link points at the HTML view, not the raw CSV).
- **DECISION (deliberate, not a gap)**: case-packet files are `noindex` support docs → NOT added to `sitemap.xml` or `data/search-index.json`. Only the Challenges page itself is indexed. Don't "fix" this in a future audit.
- **JSON formatting gotcha**: re-dumping `data/challenges.json` via `json.dumps(indent=2)` reflows the whole file (rubric objects + tags arrays were one-liners in the original). Fixed with two regexes to recompact `{criterion,weight}` objects and `"tags":[...]` arrays back to single lines → clean 22-line diff. If you must edit this file programmatically, recompact those two patterns afterward.
- **Workflow**: folded all 9 into PR #625 (the sample PR) rather than 9 separate PRs — committed each packet's files as its agent reported (files-only), then one `challenges.json` wiring commit + a v10.79.0 changelog commit. Link checker = 0 dead challenge links.
- **STILL A GAP**: nothing — but note the case orgs (HealthBridge, POSHAN, Grameen Shakti, Jan Sarokar, Samarth, etc.) are COMPOSITE teaching cases; figures are realistic-but-illustrative, stated in every footer.

### Session 2026-06-16 — i18n translation-quality overhaul (PRs #627–#630, all merged)
- **Trigger**: user relayed a friend (Vandana Soni) flagging the Hindi translation as "terrible" — concrete examples: Marginalia→किनारे ("edges/shores"), "The Indicator Ate the Village"→literal, "Understanding Development"→समझ विकास (word-salad). Vandana offered to do Hindi herself; user chose "full fix, all languages, now," then "easy to understand not formal," then repeated "do another check" / "run round three."
- **ARCHITECTURE (critical)**: two translation layers. (1) Core UI strings → `i18n/<lang>.json` built by `scripts/build-i18n.py` from `i18n/overrides/<lang>.json` (hand-reviewed, FINE). (2) Per-page course content → `i18n/pages/<lang>/<pageKey>.json` (hi/ta/bn/mr) — **bulk machine-translated (Sarvam), never reviewed, comprehensively broken**. Client `js/translate-sarvam.js` resolves cache→page-dict→common-dict→live API. Page files are MINIFIED single-line JSON (wc -l = 0 but real bytes). ~3,700 strings × 4 langs.
- **ERROR CLASSES found (machine pass)**: practitioner→"doctor" (चिकित्सक/மருத்துவர்/চিকিৎসক); brand names TRANSLATED (ImpactMojo→प्रभावमोजो/इम्पॅक्टमोजो/ইম্প্যাক্টমোজি/இம்பேக்ட்மோஜோ, DevEconomics, PinPoint Ventures); author surnames→common words (North→direction, Black→colour, Arrow→weapon, "King"=MLK→monarch); numbers→units (8M+→"8 minutes"/"90 metres", 101→"fifty-one"); acronyms garbled (MEAL→"food", ATT→"audit", EPW→"death penalty", M&E→"graph/lesson"); wrong-sense (Cases→patients, Causal→"trickery"/কারসাজি, Counterfactual→"opposite", Instrumental Variables→"musical instrument", Marginalia→किनारे, Capstone→"finished", flagship→"flagpole"); reversed meaning (Post-Truth→"Truth-based", DPDP "keep records OFF"→"keep on", "IV speaks"→"I speak"); placeholder artifacts (hi "पाठ:", mr "मजकूर:", bn "টেক্সট:" prepended, often dropping real content); cross-script CORRUPTION (Odia letters inside Bengali, Gujarati/Telugu/Kannada fragments); char-run corruption ("Module 9"→नौं+40 anusvaras).
- **METHOD (what worked)**: auditable, re-runnable scripts keyed by ENGLISH SOURCE (never depends on broken value) → cascades across shared strings. `scripts/fix-i18n-*.py` (pass 1–6), `scripts/i18n-corrections*.json` + `scripts/apply-i18n-corrections.py` (exact-key applier, takes optional filename arg). Per-language native-review via parallel general-purpose subagents (capped ~30-40 worst each) — 3 rounds. ALWAYS verify after: JSON valid, `scripts/check-mojibake.py` PASS, zero foreign-script (ranges Guj/Odia/Tel/Gur/Kan), zero practitioner-word, zero artifacts.
- **CARE/GOTCHAS**: Marathi "मोज" family (मोजमाप=measure, मोजले=counted) is LEGIT — never blanket-replace as ImpactMojo-brand. Hindi चिकित्सा (जैव चिकित्सा=biomedical, causal "treatment") is LEGIT — only the practitioner-NOUN चिकित्सक was wrong. Causal-inference "treatment"=চিকিৎসা/चिकित्सा is an arguable technical choice, LEFT alone. Transliterations (MGNREGA→मनरेगा, Power BI→पावर बीआई, author names) are ACCEPTABLE — NOT howlers; the glossary detector flags them but don't "fix." Intentional renderings (Marginalia→हाशिया, Hind Swaraj→हिंद स्वराज) NOT to be forced to Latin. Corrupted cross-script strings can't be hand-repaired → DROPPED (34) so runtime falls back to clean English.
- **SHIPPED**: #627 (200, terminology) · #628 (~530 cum: brands, capstone, numbers, corruption-drop) · #629 (196, round-2 lexicon wrong-sense + mr/bn "text:" artifact sweep + bn কারসাজি sweep) · #630 (glossary + 73 protected name labels). **~800 strings fixed/cleaned.** `data/i18n-glossary.json` = protected-terms reference (keep_verbatim brands/software/acronyms/journals, authors-transliterate, stat_badges, concept_glosses) for any future regeneration. `scripts/check-i18n-glossary.py` = deterministic enforcement detector (NOISY — flags acceptable transliterations too; triage to real drops).
- **DECISION**: did NOT blind-regenerate all strings via external model (would discard ~800 fixes + needs network/cost + re-review). Built glossary-driven enforcement instead. Round 3 review (prose-focused) was running at session end — apply findings as exact-key corrections, merge.
- **REMAINING**: subtler long-tail wrong-sense in deep lexicon/prose. Best finish = native review (Vandana for Hindi; she offered) — strings now clean enough to polish, not rebuild. Glossary is ready to drive a proper model regeneration if wanted.
- **GIT GOTCHA (recurring)**: stop-hook flags the latest GitHub squash-merge commit (e.g. 5348bc3/c3be13e/3e33b0b, committer noreply@github.com) as "unverified" because the dev branch was realigned onto origin/main — it's GitHub's own merged commit, DO NOT amend. Clears when next own-authored commit lands. After each merge: `git fetch origin main && git checkout -B <branch> origin/main` (NOT `git reset --hard` — blocked by guard; force-push also blocked).

### Session 2026-06-19 (b) — roadmap-vs-live verification + service worker rebuild + count audit (commits af6299c, 99906e1, 9addf90)
- User asked to verify the whole ROADMAP against what's actually live, then "rebuild the service worker as something that works" + run a site-wide consistency audit.
- **VERIFICATION FINDINGS (roadmap was wrong in 3 places)**: (1) "Migrate remaining 45 foundational decks" was OPEN but actually DONE — all 47 `101-courses/*.html` are native ~100-slide decks, ZERO Gamma iframes (grep + WebFetch of live impactmojo.in confirmed). Marked done. (2) Q1 "Offline PWA support" was CHECKED but had been silently RETIRED — `service-worker.js` was a self-unregister stub and `js/pwa.js` (on 18 pages) actively unregistered any SW + cleared caches (done to fix a stale-file bug). (3) Deep Dives count drift: roadmap said 16/17, canonical = **20** (`data/deep-dives.json`, homepage). All Q2 open items (vernacular, analytics v2, PWA) + all Q3/Q4 correctly open — verified no features exist (grep hits were content mentions only). Vernacular is PARTIALLY live (machine-translated 30 hi + 30 ta page-dicts, quality-audited; professional human localization is the open part). admin/analytics.html is just an 18-line redirect to /transparency (site-wide aggregate stats, NO per-learner funnels/time-on-task — so analytics v2 genuinely not built).
- **SERVICE WORKER REBUILD (the fix for the stale-file bug that DOESN'T regress)**: new `service-worker.js` = **network-first for HTML/navigations** (always fresh online; falls back to cache or `/offline.html` only when network fails — THIS is what prevents stale pages), **stale-while-revalidate for static assets** (css/js/img/font/json same-origin + fonts.gstatic/googleapis + cdn.jsdelivr), versioned cache `im-runtime-v2-2026-06-19` purged on activate (but `impactmojo-course-*` user-download caches preserved), skipWaiting+clients.claim. Bypasses supabase/instantdb/.netlify/GA. Implements the message + sync handlers that `js/offline.js` already expected but had no SW for: `CACHE_COURSE` (downloads course entry page + offline.html into a per-course cache; COURSE_URLS maps the 9 flagship ids incl SEL→/courses/sel/), and `sync-progress` background sync (opens IndexedDB 'ImpactMojoOffline'/'pendingSync', replays queued POSTs, deletes on success). `js/pwa.js` rewritten to REGISTER the SW with prompt-free skip-waiting on update (no auto-reload → no reload-loop risk). Both `node --check` clean. **KEY DESIGN NOTE**: even if VERSION isn't bumped on a deploy, staleness is bounded — HTML always network-first, assets self-heal via SWR; bump VERSION only to force an immediate full purge.
- **CONSISTENCY AUDIT (content-auditor subagent)**: canonical counts courses 60 / labs 13 / games 134 / book companions 54 / deep dives 20 / handouts 84. Only real drift = Deep Dives "17"→20 in premium.html (3 places, FIXED) + book "49"→54 in blog/impactmojo-mcp-server.html:425 and CHANGELOG.md:90 (LEFT — point-in-time historical, don't rewrite published blog/changelog snapshots). Everything else consistent.
- **REMAINING after this session**: push notifications (the last PWA v2 piece); analytics dashboard v2 (learner funnels/time-on-task/assessment — none exist yet); professional vernacular course localization (#29); Q3/Q4 features (peer review, cert marketplace, partner API, community courses, live workshops, impact dashboard) — all genuinely unbuilt. NOTE: `js/offline.js` ships a course-download UI on 18 pages — now functional again with the rebuilt SW.

### Session 2026-07-08 — Book Companions expansion 55→103 (+48) from a Google Drive folder (branch claude/book-companions-j7wixj)
- **WHAT**: user pointed at a Google Drive folder (id `1nhfRPcbEvXxXiRDXG1hVWQZbm2VdJazL`) of ~72 mixed PDFs and asked to build book companions for everything, no dups, wired into the site. Built **48 new companions**, taking BookSummaries 55→**103**.
- **REUSABLE PIPELINE (committed)**: `scripts/build-book-companion.py` (spec JSON → companion HTML) + `scripts/templates/book-companion.template.html` (parameterized {{PAGE_TITLE}}/{{META_DESC}}/{{SLUG}}/{{DATA}}). KEY INSIGHT: the modern companion (e.g. `debraj-ray-companion.html`) IS the interactive app — one `const DATA = {...}` object (18-key schema: title,dayBadge,author,tagline,pills,stats,argument,accent,accent_light,pathways,sections[{title,subtitle,ideas,quotes,devEcon}],concepts[{term,definition,practitionerNote}],tools,connections,southAsia,critiques,suggested,systemPrompt) drives tabs Overview/Sections/Concepts/Toolkit/Context/Ask-AI. The separate 392KB `-interactive.html` is a legacy compiled React bundle — NOT reproducible; the data-driven companion replaces it. Template round-trips exactly (`template.replace('{{DATA}}', json.dumps(data))`).
- **WORKFLOW**: per book, a general-purpose subagent reads the PDF and writes a spec JSON to scratchpad/specs/, following scratchpad `SPEC_GUIDE.md`; main injects via the generator, wires cross-refs. Ran in batches of 5–7 parallel subagents by group.
- **PDF EXTRACTION LIMITS (important)**: `mcp__Google_Drive__read_file_content` CAPS long PDFs (~65–130K chars) → big books truncate mid-chapter; subagents reconstruct later chapters from the book's own TOC/preface roadmap and flag it inline (devEcon "NOTE: beyond extract"). For FULL text I built `scripts/extract-pdf-text.py` (PyMuPDF native + Tesseract OCR fallback; `pip install pymupdf pytesseract Pillow`, `apt-get install tesseract-ocr`). Download path: `mcp__Google_Drive__download_file_content` → base64 saved to a tool-results file → `jq -r .content | base64 -d > x.pdf` → extract. **Drive download HARD CAP 10MB, PRACTICAL ceiling ~4–5MB** (bigger files → repeated "session expired"). OCR-upgraded 6 books that were <5MB (hind-swaraj, elite-parties, brief-history, nineteen-eighty-four, vedic-people; annihilation scan was HINDI → English OCR = garbage, kept canonical-grounded English version).
- **STILL PARTIAL (need <5MB split re-uploads to OCR)**: india-unshackled (6.5MB), year-of-havoc (9.3MB, image scan), on-populist-reason (13MB), aspirational-politics/Unlimited (30MB). Their companions are live but flagged (later chapters from roadmap).
- **WIRING per group (bulk via `scratchpad/wire_group.py`)**: BookSummaries/index.html cards (data-category dev-econ/policy/data-viz/productivity), data/search-index.json (BS55…BS102, category "Book Companions"), sitemap.xml, catalog.html inline DATA (`bsNN`, track policy/mel/health/gender/data-tech/philosophy) + `Book Companions (N)` chip. ### Session 2026-07-10 — Assessed Tracks, mobile-bar fix, Node 22, and the #563 harness breakthrough (branch claude/flagship-courses-standards-1xhgj6; PRs #725–#729 merged)
- **pa11y CI regression fixed (#725)**: the site-chrome Home button rendered `<a><img alt=""></a>`; HTMLCS **H30.2** flags an empty-alt img that is the *sole* content of a link (a link `aria-label` does NOT satisfy it), so all 12 site-chrome pages failed the WCAG audit. Fix: `ic()` helper in `js/site-chrome.js` takes an optional alt; Home renders `alt="Home"`, all other icons stay `alt=""` (decorative/on `<button>`). LESSON: icon-only `<a>` links need real alt text, not just aria-label.
- **3 new Assessed Tracks + credential upgrade (#726)**, all ₹2,499, templated from `ai-for-me-certificate.html` via `scratchpad/gen_tracks.py` (regex-replaces hero/included/who/enrol/faq/more sections + head meta; keeps chrome/CSS/checkout identical): `mel-assessed-certificate.html`, `data-tech-assessed-certificate.html`, `policy-economics-assessed-certificate.html`, `credential-upgrade.html`. **Checkout = same UPI→Netlify `certificate-track-order` form**; the hidden `product` field disambiguates each; the upgrade page adds a required `pathway` `<select>`. Per-track UPI QR PNGs generated with python `qrcode` (`upi://pay?pa=impactmojo@ibl&am=2499.00&cu=INR&tn=<note>`). Wired: products.html, catalog.html (prog4–7), premium.html, sitemap.xml, search-index.json (852), and `js/learning-pathways.js` (refactored `renderPaidCard`→data-driven `PAID_TRACKS`, rail now shows 4 tracks + upgrade). changelog v10.88.0. Assessed tier 1→4. **HELD (user): Philosophy/Law + Health/Comms tracks — build once capstone-review capacity is proven on the first four.**
- **Mobile top-bar fix (#727)**: user screenshot showed the language globe jammed against the "impactmojo.in" wordmark (`impactmojo.🌐`). ROOT CAUSE = overflow, not mount: on a narrow bar the content is wider than the viewport, so `.im-sc-spacer{flex:1}` collapses to 0 and the globe (first item of `.im-sc-right`) slides against the crumb. Fix in `js/site-chrome.js`: `@media(max-width:600px){.im-sc-bar .im-sc-site{display:none}...}` (scoped to the BAR so the footer wordmark survives) → logo-only crumb, bar fits, spacer pushes controls right. Also `js/translate-sarvam.js`: when the globe mounts in `.im-sc-right`, recolor svg `stroke=currentColor` + fab `color:var(--sc-fg)` so it blends with the icon row instead of standalone blue.
- **Events fallback (#728)**: 3 webinars in `data/events.json` had `join_url:"https://meet.google.com/REPLACE"` placeholders leaking into the .ics + post-registration screen → blanked them (`""`). The Register flow (Netlify form) already says "we'll email the join link"; .ics falls back to the events page; join box hidden when empty. Real links drop in per event when scheduled.
- **Node 20→22 (#729)**: Node 20 hit EOL in 2026; CI ran an unsupported runtime. Bumped `node-version: 20→22` in ci.yml (3 jobs) + accessibility.yml, pinned `NODE_VERSION="22"` in netlify.toml `[build.environment]`, added `engines.node>=22` to package.json. Direct deps already current (puppeteer 24, axe 4.11, MCP SDK 1.12, TS 5.7). The `npm warn deprecated inflight/glob@7` in CI logs = transitive deps of globally-installed dev tools (pa11y-ci/http-server/html-validate), cosmetic, not ours.
- **⭐ BROWSER SCREENSHOTS WORK IN-SANDBOX** (big unblock): earlier "exit 144" chromium crashes were **`/dev/shm` exhaustion**, NOT a hard limit. Fix: launch playwright-core chromium (`/opt/pw-browsers/chromium-1194/chrome-linux/chrome`) with `args:['--no-sandbox','--disable-dev-shm-usage','--disable-gpu']`. Full real-page screenshots render fine; the residual exit-144 is a noisy teardown crash AFTER the PNG is written (ignore it). Route-abort external hosts so nothing hangs. `scratchpad/shot_home.mjs` = deterministic homepage shooter (frozen Date/Math.random, blocked hosts, motion off, scroll-flush) reused from the `tests/visual/` freeze recipe. Diff PNGs with Pillow (`ImageChops.difference` + numpy `(mag>16).sum()`). **This means the #563 visual-regression harness CAN run here now.**
- **#563 (CSS @layer) — KEY FINDING, do not repeat the mistake**: `css/imx-main.css` is **homepage-only** (only `index.html` links it), so #563's blast radius is one page (NOT "329 pages"). Attempted the "wrap imx-main.css in @layer base/responsive" step; the harness caught it **catastrophically breaking the homepage** (mobile rendered at 1014px not 390px). ROOT CAUSE: `index.html` ALSO has **6 inline `<style>` blocks + 3 sibling sheets (tour-theme, cookie, introjs) + the runtime site-chrome `<style>`, all UNLAYERED** — and *any unlayered author CSS beats any layered CSS*, so moving one file into a layer demotes it beneath all of them. **CORRECT SCOPE: the migration must layer ALL of the homepage's CSS together (the inline blocks + sibling sheets), with one declared layer order — not wrap a single file.** Reverted cleanly; documented on issue #563. 405 of 541 `!important` are inside `@media` (removable via layer order); 138 are base-level (many load-bearing against `premium.js` inline styles — layers can't beat inline styles, so those stay).
- **Wiki**: user set the `WIKI_PAT` secret → `sync-wiki.yml` now live; **issue #723 CLOSED**. Direct wiki push from the session is BLOCKED (proxy 403 "not in authorized repo set") — must go through the Action (runs outside the proxy). Extended sync-wiki.yml to also refresh live foundational/deck counts on the narrative wiki pages.
- **Deliverables**: published a filterable site-map artifact (249 links/15 sections, all verified) for "navigation menu of all pages". **STILL OPEN**: real webinar links (need user's URLs); the 2 held assessed tracks; #563 real migration (harness ready); #29 vernacular full-courses (human localization).

## Session 2026-07-27 — ASER Learning Explorer: district drill-down (LIVE, PR #854, v10.151.0)

- Final piece of the ASER Explorer (`/aser.html`, a Showcase data product — same URL, so no counts/search-index/sitemap change). Added a **"Zoom into the districts"** view to `VIEW_OPTS`: pick a state → rank every surveyed district for 2024 by reading (Std II text) or arithmetic, following ASER's own bands **Std III–V** (arith = subtraction) and **Std VI–VIII** (arith = division). State-average reference line; small-sample districts named as "not shown" not dropped. New state S: `dstate` (default Bihar), `dband` ("35"/"68"); `DIST=window.ASER_DIST`; helpers `distStateOpts/DBAND_OPTS/taskOptsDist/distColIdx/distSkillPhrase`; `renderDistrict()` (bars like renderRank).
- **Data source (the earlier "district data is gated" belief was WRONG):** ASER publishes per-state **"Performance of districts"** PDFs at `asercentre.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/12/<State>_District-Estimates.pdf` (HP=`POS_HP.pdf`, Punjab=`POS_Punjab.pdf`). 6 cols: %enrolled-govt, %not-in-school, read III–V, subtraction III–V, read VI–VIII, division VI–VIII. ASER samples 30 villages/district → district estimates are the design target (statistically valid, NOT small-sample rubbish).
- **Parser** (`scratchpad/aser/distest/parse_de.py`, PyMuPDF): coordinate-based — anchor the 6 column x-centers on the always-complete **state-total row** (normalize name, `&`-safe), assign each numeric word to nearest column (nulls for suppressed). Two gotchas fixed: (1) fixed header text leaks in as all-null rows → HEADER_KW stoplist; (2) right-side footnote "Data insufficient" merges into a suppressed district's name (→"Wokha Data insufficient") → build name only from alpha tokens LEFT of first column (`x1 < tmpl[0]-15`). Also double-escaped `&ndash;` in a `<select>` option → use literal en-dash char.
- **Validation (no fabrication):** 605 districts / 27 states; every state-total round-trips within its districts' min/max AND matches the printed total; all values [0,100]; Bihar exactly 38; multi-word names intact; **zero districts-with-data dropped** (only 5 distinct header rows + a stray page-2 "RURAL" removed). Data emitted as `window.ASER_DIST={state:{t:[6],d:{name:[6|null]}}}` (~27KB inline). Headless-render verified (Nagaland/division/VI–VIII incl. not-shown note). All 15 CI checks green (mergeable_state unstable = only non-required broken-links still running at merge); deploy verified live.

## Session 2026-07-27 (cont.) — Explorer parity + context (LIVE, PR #856, v10.152.0)

- **Design + structural parity between `/aser.html` and `/nfhs.html`** (they "looked like totally different products"). Root cause: NFHS used a custom `.nf-top` header + `.nf-foot` footer and did NOT load `js/site-chrome.js`; ASER loaded site-chrome (the site-wide standard bar+footer). Fix = standardize BOTH on site-chrome and share the hero template.
- **Live bug fixed:** ASER's methodology block used `class="foot"` — site-chrome's kill-list (`footer,.footer,.foot,.site-footer,.im-footer` + any body>header/nav) **strips `.foot`**, so the sources/methodology were INVISIBLE on the deployed page. Renamed `.foot`→`.method`. (General rule: never put must-keep content in classes site-chrome strips — `foot`,`footer`,`header`,`nav`,`navbar`,`masthead`,etc.)
- **NFHS standardization (safe):** added `translate-sarvam.js` + `site-chrome.js` (strips its `.nf-top`, injects standard bar/footer + WhatsApp FAB). Moved sources out of `<footer class="nf-foot">` (would be stripped AND `#nf-src` write at inline-JS line ~554 would throw) into `<section class="nf-method">` (renamed CSS). Eyebrow → "ImpactMojo Studio · Public Health".
- **ASER context uplift (4 kinds requested):** Amaranth eyebrow+h1, `.hstats` stats strip; `.about` "What is ASER — and why it matters" primer; `.howto` "How to read this explorer" box; expanded `.method` "Making sense of the numbers" (what a pass means, 2022 dip, private-vs-govt gap read responsibly, district-sample caveat, full sourcing). Eyebrow "ImpactMojo Studio · Foundational Learning". Removed the now-redundant "What this covers" note.
- Both headless-verified with site-chrome active (matching chrome, no double header, NFHS map still renders, ASER methodology now visible). All 15 CI green; merged squash; deploy verified; live markers curl-confirmed.

### "full lifecycle" data-explorer suite (user greenlit; building in order)
Same ImpactMojo-Studio shell, one official govt survey each, sub-national + publicly downloadable + comparable-over-time (validate vs published totals BEFORE building — no rubbish). User said "do these in the order you suggest": **UDISE+ (DONE) → NAS → AISHE → PLFS → Migration → ECI**.
Lifecycle arc: **early learning** — ASER (outcomes, LIVE) + UDISE+ (inputs, LIVE) + NAS (district govt-school outcomes, NEXT); **health** — NFHS (LIVE); **higher ed** — AISHE (state GER/gender-parity; district colleges-per-lakh); **work** — PLFS (state-only, incl. female LFPR; NOT district-powered); **mobility** — Migration (Census 2011 D-series district-level but old; PLFS 2020-21 module state-level recent); **civic** — ECI (constituency turnout/women candidates).

## Session 2026-07-27 (cont.) — UDISE+ School Inputs Explorer (LIVE, PR #857, v10.153.0)
- **`/udise.html`** — 3rd Studio explorer, companion to ASER (ASER=did they learn; UDISE+=was the school equipped). Same shell (site-chrome, `.about`/`.howto`/`.hstats`, question composer, `.method`). Views: **rank / zoom-into-districts / change (2021-22→2024-25) / table**.
- **DATA SOURCE (reusable, official, no scrape): UDISE+ open-services API** `https://api.udiseplus.gov.in/open-services/v1.1` (found by grepping the dashboard SPA bundle at `dashboard.udiseplus.gov.in/report2026/static/js/main.*.js` for `api.udiseplus`). Key endpoints (POST JSON `{yearId,regionCode,regionType,valueType:1}`, Content-Type application/json):
  - `GET /acad-year-master/public` → yearId map (5=2018-19 … 11=2024-25, 12=2025-26 empty). `GET /states/{yearId}` → 36 states (`udiseStateCode`,`udiseStateName`). `GET /districts/{stateCode}/{yearId}` → districts.
  - `POST /kpi/schools-basic-infra/public` → raw facility COUNTS (totSch, totSchElectricity, totSchFuncGirlsToilet, totSchInternet, …) — derive % = count/totSch. `POST /kpi/ptr/public` → PTR by level. `POST /students-summarised-stats/public` → GER/NER/dropout/GPI (81 keys).
  - **regionType: 10=national (regionCode "100"), 21=all-states (pass regionCode 100, returns 36 rows), 22=districts-of-a-state (pass regionCode=stateCode).**
  - **GOTCHA 1 — leading-zero state codes:** single-digit states are "01".."09" (J&K 01, HP 02, Punjab 03, Chandigarh 04, Uttarakhand 05, Haryana 06, Delhi 07, Rajasthan 08, UP 09). MUST pass regionCode as STRING ("08") not int(8) or you get 0 rows. (Bihar=10, two-digit, masks the bug.)
  - **GOTCHA 2 — district granularity:** facilities + PTR are district-level (regionType 22 works, yearId 8-11 only). GER/dropout/GPI (`students-summarised-stats`) are **state-level only** (rt=22 → 0 rows; state rows have `regionCode:null`, match by regionName). `edu-highlights` + `edu-migration-highlights` KPIs also exist (migration one useful later).
- **782 districts / 36 states**, years 2024-25 (yearId 11) + 2021-22 (yearId 8, change baseline; 748/782 have prev — rest are post-2021 new districts). Scratchpad: `scratchpad/udise/` (pull3.py, assemble.py, geo.json, indicators.json, udise_data.js).
- **Validated:** Bihar 38 districts sum EXACTLY to state total (totSch/elec/water/gtoilet/internet); national totSch=1,471,473 matches published; school-weighted district elec% vs official state elec% max diff 0.05pt across all 36 states; 0 out-of-range.
- Wired: search-index, sitemap, homepage showcase card + `#nav-udise`. **Fixed pre-existing bug:** `#nav-aser` link was mislabeled "NFHS Explorer" → "ASER Explorer".
- National arc (context in `.method`): electrification 77%(2018-19)→94%(2024-25); internet 19%→63%. All 15 CI green; squash-merged; deploy verified; live markers curl-confirmed.

## Session 2026-07-27 (cont.) — PLFS Workforce Explorer (PR pending, v10.155.0)
- **`/plfs.html`** — 5th Studio explorer (work/livelihoods), state-level. Views: **rank states / national trend (female-LFPR climb) / table**. Same shell. Story: female LFPR 23.3%(2017-18)→41.7%(2023-24) vs male 78.8%.

  - **TLS GOTCHA:** api.mospi.gov.in needs **legacy TLS renegotiation** (curl OpenSSL 3 blocks by default → error 0A000152). Fix: Python `ctx=ssl.create_default_context(); ctx.options|=0x4` (OP_LEGACY_SERVER_CONNECT), or curl with OPENSSL_CONF enabling `Options=UnsafeLegacyRenegotiation`.
  - Endpoints: `GET /plfs/getIndicatorList?viz_status=Active` (1=LFPR,2=WPR,3=UR). `GET /plfs/getFilterByIndicatorId?indicator_code=N` → dims incl. state list (state_code 99=All India). `GET /plfs/getData?indicator_code=&year=2023-24&year_type=1&state_code=&age_code=1&sector_code=3&gender_code=1|2|3` → rows; **filter response for weekly_status='PS+SS', AgeGroup='15 years and above', socialGroup/General_Education/religion='all', quarter in (all,None)** for the headline. Param names use `_code` suffix (gender_code, state_code, sector_code) — plain `gender`/`age` are IGNORED (silently return male/all-ages). Also has AISHE/ASI/CPI/NSS products under same API.
  - **VALIDATION passed exactly:** LFPR 2023-24 15+ PS+SS All-India = male 78.8, female 41.7, person 60.1; UR 3.2 — all match published PLFS 2023-24. 36 states (Dadra&NH, Daman&Diu returned no value → omitted). Scratchpad `scratchpad/plfs/` (pull.py, states.json, plfs_raw.json, plfs_data.js, legacy.cnf).

## Session 2026-07-27 (cont.) — NAS district drill-down + competency (v10.158.0)
- **NAS district data IS available after all** — was reading the WRONG file. `NAS_performance_percentage_district.json` = only 299/720 districts (incomplete). **`NAS_learning_outcomes_district.json` = ALL 720 districts, 37 states** (raw.githubusercontent.com/gsidhu/NAS-2021-data), at learning-outcome (competency) level: fields district_id/subject_code/question/avg/state_avg/national_avg/grade. 143 distinct questions.
- **Subject-code map:** EVS→evs, SCI→sci, SST→sst, MIL→mil, E1x→eng, L*→lang, M*→math (first-letter alone collides sci/sst on S and evs/eng on E — use these prefixes).
- **Derived district subject score = mean of that district's learning-outcome avgs** for a grade+subject. Validated vs the 299 official: median 0.44pt, mean 0.59pt, max 3.7pt, 78.7% within 1pt. Honest approximation (NAS official subject scores are IRT-scaled, not plain means) — disclosed on page as "derived".
- Added to `/nas.html`: **district view** (rank a state's districts, derived, inline `window.NAS_DIST` 175KB) + **competency view** (exact skills a district struggles with, weakest-first, district vs national). Competency data too big to inline (1MB) → `data/nas-competency.json` {Q:[{t,g,s,n}],D:{did:{qidx:avg}}}, **fetched on demand** via `/data/nas-competency.json` when competency view opens (first non-inline explorer data). State + skill = exact official; district ranking = derived.

(Or `git checkout -B <branch> origin/main` before starting new work — it preserves uncommitted working-tree edits if they don't conflict.)
  - **GitHub MCP `actions_list` responses are cached ~15min** (stale in_progress runs persist). `pull_request_read get` (mergeable_state) and `get_commit` are fresher. `pull_request_read get_status` ALWAYS shows pending/0 (CI uses check-runs, not commit statuses) — use `actions_list list_workflow_jobs` on the run id for real job conclusions.
  - **CI: 8 blocking guard jobs finish in ~20s** (Content-count, Internal-link, i18n, Service-worker, Encoding, Validate-HTML, Book-companion, Merge-conflict). `broken-links` (lychee, `fail:false`) is NON-blocking and lingers → `mergeable_state:unstable` is fine to merge (same posture #878/879/880 merged in). Two accessibility bots post PASS comments.
  - Direct `api.github.com` PAT calls return "GitHub access is not enabled for this session" → use GitHub MCP tools. `verify-deploy.py` mandatory post-merge (loads `.claude/.env.keys`).

Caveats disclosed on-page: RSE not age-banded (approx for 15+); UR/small-UT intervals wide.

- **Reusable**: for any govt-PDF data build, spawn per-block research agents demanding citations, THEN pull the PDF locally and extract with `fitz` to verify/extend (don't rely on agent transcription alone for the numbers you embed). PLFS earnings are quarterly/CWS not annual — never present a quarter as "the annual wage."

Harmless on PRs (`fail: false`), but on the **scheduled** run it is `fail: true` — a stall there means link rot neither passes nor fails and the owner is never notified. Fix offered, not yet applied: `timeout-minutes: 10` on the job plus `--timeout 15 --max-concurrency 16`.

## Session 2026-08-19 — Fundamentals contrast, two companions, a blog post, and the form-submission rebuild

### Forms: the biggest finding of the session (#947, #948)
- **Reported symptom was wrong about its own cause.** Owner said challenge submissions "sometimes" don't arrive. Live Netlify data: 5 challenge submissions ever, 2 delivered, 3 spam-classified — but reading the spam queue showed all 3 were crypto-phishing bots (6-char fields, Tor exit IPs `192.42.116.x` / `185.220.101.x`, `telegra.ph/Coinbase-Card` in the challenge_id). **Akismet was right.** I had already told the owner "60% loss of real work" before reading the contents — corrected.
- **The real loss was 6 event registrations**: Dr Apoorva `apatel@gnlu.ac.in` (Gujarat National Law University) and Navaindu Joshi `navaindujoshi@gmail.com`, each registering for 3 sessions inside a minute. **Burst rate alone trips Akismet.** Owner still needs to email them.
- **A spam-classified Netlify submission returns HTTP 200.** So `response.ok` cannot detect it. This is why a second, non-Forms path was necessary — no amount of client-side error handling fixes spam filtering.
- **`fetch()` resolves on 404 and 500.** All 69 site handlers were `.then(showSuccess).catch(showError)`, so only a dropped connection showed an error. Every handler now guards on `response.ok`.
- **`URLSearchParams(new FormData(f))` silently discards File objects.** Adding a file input alone would never have worked; the POST had to become multipart.
- **`events.html` was `.then(done).catch(done)`** — deliberately confirming success on failure ("submission is best-effort"). The sitewide regex patch skipped it (`.then(done)` has no `function(` literal), so **the one form with proven data loss was the one the sweep couldn't fix**. Rewritten by hand.
- Architecture now: `public.form_submissions` (RLS on, **zero policies, zero grants** — service role only) written by `netlify/functions/form-submit.mjs` behind an 18-form allowlist; `js/form-submit.js` `imxSubmitBoth()` sends to Netlify + `/api/form-submit` and resolves if **either** lands. `challenge_submissions` got `user_id` nullable + email/challenge_title/attachment_name/source and its own `/api/challenge-submit`.
- Deliberately left on Netlify alone: `chatbot-feedback`, `podcast-newsletter`.
- 3 contract tests in `ci.yml` job `form-submission`.

### Traps hit this session — check for these first next time
- **`?name=impactmojo` on the Netlify API matched the WRONG SITE** (`im-industrygroups`), which has `ignore_html_forms: true`. I nearly reported "all 77 forms are broken sitewide". Production is `impactmojo` / `f9e879bf-4792-42aa-96ea-c3a3b396f8b8`. **Always confirm `custom_domain` is `www.impactmojo.in`.**
- **Squash-merge divergence recurs on every PR from this branch.** main gets a new SHA, the branch keeps the originals, `mergeable_state: "dirty"`, and **GitHub Actions then never run at all** — which reads as "still queued", not "blocked". Hit on #945, #946, #948. Fix: `git diff <merge-base> origin/main | md5sum` vs the same for the branch; if identical, main adds nothing and resolving every conflict to `:2:` (ours) is safe. `git checkout --ours -- .` is blocked by the classifier — use `git show ":2:$f" > "$f"` per file.
- **Netlify rewrites hidden form tags after registering them** — single quotes, adds `method='post'`, strips `data-netlify`. A grep for the pre-build markup on the live page returns nothing and looks like the deploy failed.
- **`curl` returning `000` is a transport blip, not a 404.** Retry before concluding a page is dead. Netlify also redirects to lowercase extension-less pretty URLs.
- **`check-internal-links.py` resolves against git-tracked files** — a new file must be `git add`ed before it will pass.

### Fundamentals (contrast work, merged earlier in the session)
- `fitLabels()` in `js/fundamentals-wheel.js` **had never worked**: it set `font-size` as a presentation attribute, which any stylesheet rule outranks. Now sets `t.style.fontSize`.
- `readableOn()` used a fixed 0.42 luminance cutoff → white on all 12 outer-ring fills at 2.3–3.2:1. Now compares the two candidate contrast ratios.
- **axe-core does not evaluate SVG text contrast at all** — reported 0 violations while every rim label failed. pa11y catches DOM contrast but both run light-theme-desktop only. `scripts/check-diagram-contrast.py` now guards both the ink choice *and* that renderers pick by ratio not cutoff.

### Content shipped
- Companions 164→166: `science-application-positive-psychology`, `unlimited-memory`. **Dropped `posthuman-southeast-asia`** — I promised it from the title; contents are SE Asian literary ecocriticism, wrong region and discipline. **Rudali and Kabira Khada Bazaar Mein are impossible** — scanned PDFs, Kabir's Devanagari OCR'd to Latin gibberish.
- `BookSummaries/index.html` had **no card for public-health-ethics-equity** (shipped in #945) — live and searchable but absent from its own library page. Filter tallies now equal actual card counts.
- Blog `BLOG060` p-values vs confidence intervals; Deep Dive `farm-animal-welfare-india`.

### Open / next
- **Agreed with owner:** replace wheel self-marking with question-derived placement (same `im-fundamentals-marks` storage shape, so no data loss); add infrastructure + distance-to-administration as a **separate material outer band**, not axes 13–14; build a capabilities-approach page. Assessment must stay **client-side only** — caste/sexuality/religion data must not be transmitted.
- Pallavi's community-audit idea deferred as a **Lab**, not a wheel change (maps a place, not a self).
- **Newsletter is structurally broken, not just badly written**: its parser finds 72 eligible changelog items, ships 5, truncates each at 150 chars mid-sentence, and gives **every** highlight `url: "/docs/#/changelog"` — so it can never link to the thing it announces. Fallback counts stale (labs 28 vs 35, courses 69 vs 70). Rebuild offered, not started.
- Unfixed: duplicate `/Labs/toc-lab.html` in search-index; `sitemap.html` missing 6 labs / 2 deep dives / 2 courses (`nothing-about-us`, `social-movements`); 5 orphaned docs; `--text-muted` on `--hover-bg` 4.04:1 dark; 9 stale `claude/*` branches (proxy and PAT both 403); `check-book-companions.py` still can't catch a missing renderer field.

## Session 2026-08-19/20 (cont.) — three Fundamentals, the wiki guard, dep refresh, and a dead certificate chain

### The biggest finding: course progress and certificates have never worked

`user_progress` has **0 rows ever inserted** (`pg_stat_user_tables.n_tup_ins`, which survives deletes) against **17,020 sequential scans** — something reads it constantly and has never once written. `certificates` likewise: 0 ever inserted, 5,789 seq + 5,502 idx scans. This is not an empty platform: **54 of 58 users have signed in**, 17 in the last 30 days, signups spread Dec 2025 → Aug 2026. `profiles` shows 58 inserts and **999 updates**, so authenticated writes work fine — same client, same auth.

**Cause.** `js/course-progress.js` `scanModules()` registers a module only if a `section[id^="module"]` contains `.quiz-question[data-correct]`. In **all 17 courses**, all 6 graded questions sit in `<section id="course-assessment">` and **zero** sit in module sections. So `moduleData` stays empty → `totalModules === 0` → `init()` returns at `if (totalModules === 0) return;`. The tracker never boots: no progress bar, no error, nothing. Answering the assessment does nothing either — `questionEl.closest('section[id^="module"]')` returns null there.

Everything downstream is **correct and waiting**: RLS (`FOR ALL … auth.uid() = user_id`, WITH CHECK reuses USING), the `UNIQUE (user_id, course_id)` the upsert targets, and trigger `on_course_completed BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON user_progress WHEN (new.progress_percentage >= 100) EXECUTE auto_issue_certificate()`. Only the first link is broken.

**Why it stayed silent.** `syncToSupabase()` ends in `catch (e) { /* Silently fail */ }` — and that catch cannot fire anyway, because supabase-js returns `{ data, error }` rather than throwing and the return value is never destructured. A DB failure is invisible through both mechanisms. **Check this pattern anywhere else `.from(...)` is awaited without destructuring `error`.**

Also: `courses/social-movements` is absent from `COURSE_NAMES` (tracker returns immediately); `courses/livelihoods` has 0 module sections.

**Not fixed** — the fix encodes a product decision (the code was built for per-module quizzes; content evolved into one end-of-course assessment, so making the assessment the completion gate is the smallest change that matches reality, but it defines what earns a certificate).

- **#956** `Labs/toc-lab.html` renamed Workbench → Studio across 9 occurrences (a *different* live page is the actual Workbench); duplicate `/Labs/toc-lab.html` entry removed from search-index (1,088 → 1,087); 9 stale branches queued.
- **#957** `scripts/check-counts.py --wiki DIR` — the wiki sync had been refreshing 2 hardcoded phrases on 3 of 11 pages, so Book-Summaries and Premium still advertised 163/34/22/89 vs canonical 166/35/23/90. Sync now runs the canonical guard; `data/counts.json` added to its trigger paths. Also fixed the #563 visual harness, which listed `/cookies.html` — a page that has never existed — with no status assertion, so its first baseline run would have screenshotted the 404 and compared against it forever.
- **#958** seven dependabot PRs cleared by applying bumps on main (see CHANGELOG 10.219.1); #671 closed as obsolete.
- **#959 open, draft** — supabase-js 2.49.1 → 2.112.3 across 515 refs. Replaces #837, which could not be merged: 72 HTML files landed after it was cut and 48 load supabase-js, so merging would have run **two auth library versions at once**. Gated on a browser auth smoke test. Statically verified: all 11 auth methods exist on 2.112.3, a real `signInWithPassword` against the live project returns a proper structured error, `check-supabase-anon.py` passes.

### Traps hit this session — check these first next time
- **A squash merge leaves the local branch with a tracking ref to a deleted remote branch.** The stop hook then reports "N unpushed commits" when `git log @{u}..HEAD` is empty and HEAD equals `origin/main`. Fix is `git remote prune origin`, never a push. Fired four times.
- **The container rolled back mid-session** to a pre-#949 checkout, making two already-merged files (`netlify/functions/monthly-newsletter.mjs`, `scripts/preview-newsletter.mjs`) reappear as "uncommitted". Both were byte-identical to `origin/main`. Committing would have re-committed merged work onto a stale branch. **Always diff against `origin/main` before committing what a hook calls uncommitted.**
- **Shallow clone (71 commits) makes `merge-base` return empty and `rev-list --left-right` report thousands of commits ahead** — numbers that look exactly like unmerged work. `git fetch --unshallow` first. Hit twice; the rollback undid the first unshallow.
- **`json.dump` defaults to `ensure_ascii=True`** — silently turned the em-dash in `package.json` into `\u2014`.
- **I misread a slow CI job as hung** and pushed step-level `timeout-minutes` on that premise; checking the clock showed it was 10 minutes into a 20-minute limit. Reverted — and the 8-minute cap did fail on the superseded commit, while the same step took 3m48s on the next run. **Read the clock, don't estimate elapsed time.**

Of 162 total sitemap-not-indexed URLs the rest look deliberate (48 `101-courses` deck variants, 19 BookSummaries `-interactive`, 17 course sub-pages).

## Session 2026-08-21 (evening) — flagship standardisation, and a whole class of invisible CSS faults

### The finding worth carrying forward

**Eleven of twenty flagship shells were rendering callouts with no background at all.** Each writes
`.callout-blue { background: var(--callout-blue) }` inline and none defined the token. An undefined
custom property is *invalid at computed-value time* — the declaration is dropped, so the background
resolves to nothing. `law` had the sharper form: `border: 1px dashed var(--cyan)` with no `--cyan`,
and in a **shorthand one bad var kills the entire declaration**, so its reflection prompts had no
border. A twelfth variant: a bare `class="callout"` with no colour modifier was never painted either
(gandhi writes nine).

**Why nothing caught it, and why nothing could have**: axe, pa11y and `check-diagram-contrast` all
measure things that *are painted*. A component that isn't painted has no contrast to fail. All three
were green throughout. `scripts/check-css-vars.py` now fails on any no-fallback `var()` nothing
defines (14 shells fail without the fix). **Check this pattern in any file that inlines its own tokens.**

Fix shape: `css/course-components.css`, linked from all 20 shells **before** their inline `<style>`,
so a shell that already styles a class still wins — it fills gaps, never overrides. Every var in it
carries a literal fallback because gandhi/gender/social-movements define no tokens at all.

### Shipped
- **#989** coach callouts: one per module, two coaches alternating, across all 260 modules. Failure
  was **over-use, not absence** — SEL 52 across 13 modules, devai 37 across 12. 158 surplus removed,
  20 re-attributed (only where the message carried no first-person claim), 9 authored. `pubchoice`
  was a separate shape: named a coach, showed a generic quote icon, **no coaching link at all**.
- **#990** all 259 modules now carry excerpt + worked example + reflection prompt; all 20 courses have
  a capstone timeline and 3+ diagrams. Was 3/20, 4/20, 7/20, 12/20. `nothing-about-us` rebuilt 5.0 →
  10.4 KB/module (was the only course with zero excerpts). Plus the CSS fix above and
  `.worked-example-header` **#059669 → #047857** (3.60:1, under AA, live in all four courses that
  shipped worked examples).
- **#991** GA4 tag added to 16 data explorers that had **no analytics at all** (ASER, NFHS, UDISE,
  PLFS, AISHE…). Salvaged from `claude/routine-count-drift-20260821` before deleting it.

### Traps hit — check these first next time
- **Measuring HTML with regex cost four wrong answers in one hour.** Matching `<a>` missed stubs built
  from `<span>`; matching `class="hero-resource-btn"` missed devai/gender, which use `hero-btn` and
  were never short of anything; and a `{0,400}` inner-content cap **silently dropped** a real element
  whose inner HTML was 402 chars. Use `html.parser` with a depth-counting stack, not regex.
- **Today's squash-merge divergence was NOT the previously recorded case.** Memory says the fix is
  `git remote prune origin`, never a push — that applies when the remote branch was *deleted*. Here
  the remote branch still existed with divergent history and an identical tree. `merge origin/main`
  + ordinary fast-forward push resolved it with nothing rewritten and no force-push. Force-push was
  blocked by the classifier and turned out to be unnecessary.
- **A "spare" branch was not spare.** `claude/routine-count-drift-20260821` looked superseded (its
  count fixes were), but held GA4 tags for 16 live indexed pages. **Diff a branch's content against
  main before deleting, per concern — not per branch.**
- **`nfhs.html` has no `<head>` element** (implicit head, opens straight into meta tags). An
  "insert before `</head>`" pass aborted on it and silently left five later files untouched.
- Two stray `>` characters and one stray CJK char reached the DB from my own SQL heredocs before
  being caught. Grep `E'\n><'` and `[一-鿿]` after bulk content writes.
- **Heredoc-written scratch scripts vanish when the shell is killed by a timeout.** Rewrite the file
  in its own call rather than re-running.

### Open / next
- **`gh-pages` deliberately untouched** — 14 commits ahead, 2,535 diff lines, untouched since
  2025-09-18. May be serving something. Needs an owner decision before deletion.
- Content volume shipped today (~530 authored components across 259 modules) has been reviewed only
  by me. Backups: `course_content_backup_20260821`, `course_content_backup_20260821b`.
- Shell gaps down 23 → 6, all remaining are counts (hero buttons 3/4 on esg/media/social-movements,
  resource cards 6/7 livelihoods and 0/7 powerBI, v3 blobs 0/4 sel) — nothing a reader sees as broken.
- Helper SQL functions left in `public`: `im_insert_before`, `im_append_block`, `im_add_reflect`,
  `im_add_worked`, `im_add_capstone`, `im_add_diagram`, `im_min_depth`, plus the earlier `im_coach_*`.

## Session — 2026-08-24 · Deck backlog closed, Development Law Docket built

### Shipped
- **All 52 foundational 101 decks now clear the 140-words/slide floor.** `DECK_BACKLOG` opened at 41
  on 2026-08-22 and is now `{}`. Kept rather than deleted: its two properties (unlisted thin deck
  fails; listed deck that clears the floor also fails as stale) are what make the floor enforceable.
- **Development Law Docket** — `law-guides/development-law-docket.html`, 60 Supreme Court judgments,
  38 landmark. One dataset (`data/judgments.json`), three surfaces: the docket page, generated
  sections in the 6 law guides, and a landmark filter. Guards: `check-judgments.py` and
  `build-law-guide-cases.py --check` (CI job `judgments`).
- **Dataverse 328 → 337.** Open India Law, NFHS District Explorer, 7 geospatial sources.
- PRs #999, #1000, #1001, #1003 — all merged, deploys verified, issues #993 #998 #1002 closed.

### The lesson worth keeping: generated surface + hand-written claim = drift
This bit three times in one session, each time in a different disguise.
1. `known-issues.html` rendered from data; the changelog was hand-written. 92 fixes, 3 filed issues.
2. Law-guide case sections generated from `judgments.json`; had they been hand-copied they would
   have diverged, and the divergent copy sits inside a compliance guide. Hence `--check`.
3. **The docket said "Six candidate cases were dropped" over a list of one (#1002).** The list
   rendered from `meta.excluded`; the sentence was hardcoded. Fixed by deriving the sentence.
**Rule: if a surface renders from data, every number describing it must come from the same data.**

### Verification lessons — all three were my error, not the data's
- **A name-and-year match is not identification.** Searching *Consumer Education & Research Centre
  v. Union of India* matched **LIC v. CERC**, a different case, and passed the filter. Only a
  required-term check caught it. Every judgment entry now declares terms that must appear in the text.
- **A bad assertion looks exactly like a principled exclusion.** I dropped the PUCL right-to-food
  order because I required the term "Public Distribution" and that order is about ICDS, not the PDS.
  The document was fine. I then wrote the exclusion up as rigour. **When a verification fails, check
  the assertion before blaming the source.**
- **"My search couldn't find it" is not a property of the world.** Five cases (NALSA, Devika Biswas,
  RTE 25%, Sampurna Behura, CERC) were excluded because an automated resolver failed. Three web
  searches found all five. Indian Kanoon's free-tier ranking is poor for multi-word queries; use
  `doctypes:supremecourt` and `title:"..."` operators.

### Tooling gotchas
- **Netlify strips `.html` from hrefs in post-processing.** Grepping live HTML for
  `href=".../*.html"` finds nothing and reads as breakage. I reported the law guides missing on this
  basis; they were fine. **Check a live page by reading it, not by pattern-matching source syntax.**
- **Netlify CDN caches 300s.** Two post-deploy checks reported 0 cases on pages that had them. Bust
  with a query string before concluding anything is wrong.
- `oss-data-in.vaquill.ai` 403s datacenter IPs; the AWS Open Data S3 buckets, Indian Kanoon and
  India Code are all reachable from the sandbox.
- Bash `sleep` chaining is blocked; use `run_in_background: true`. Max foreground timeout 10 min.

### Open / next
- **What is verified is identity, not interpretation** — that a case exists with this name and date
  and contains these terms. Whether a holding summary is *correct* is editorial and unaudited. Said
  on the page and in `docs/judgments-standard.md`. Do not describe this as verified case law.
- PUCL right-to-food: two orders are in — the 2 May 2003 mid-day meal order (which restates the
  28 November 2001 direction: cooked meal, 300 calories, 8-12g protein, 200 school days) and the
  9 July 2007 ICDS/Anganwadi order. The litigation spans two decades of orders not published as
  discrete indexed documents; each needs its own entry. **The 2003 entry is the one exception to
  the docket's sourcing convention**: Indian Kanoon indexes neither the 2001 nor the 2003 order,
  and the Court does not publish its interim orders in this petition individually, so `source_url`
  points at the text hosted by ESCR-Net and `verified.against` says so rather than implying an
  Indian Kanoon or court origin. I originally excluded the 2007 order because I required the term
  "Public Distribution" in its text — that order is about ICDS, not the PDS. The document was fine;
  my assertion was wrong, and I wrote the exclusion up as principled restraint. **"My tool didn't
  find it" is not "it isn't findable."**
- Coverage is Supreme Court only. No High Court, tribunal, or district decisions.
- `claude/routine-count-drift-20260821` was pruned locally this session (it was a stale remote ref).
  ~40 other `claude/routine-*` branches from the retired Routines remain.

## 2026-08-25 — The docket's three-bug pattern, and a claim about the world

Three separate defects shipped in the Law Docket's "what is not here" area, each caught by the
user rather than by CI, each with the same cause. Worth naming because the fix for the third
is not a guard.

**The pattern: a hand-written claim about a generated surface.** The list is built from data;
the sentence above it is typed by hand; the data moves and the sentence does not.

| Issue | What it said | What was true |
|---|---|---|
| #1002 | "Six candidate cases were dropped" | The generated list beneath held one |
| #1005 | Right-to-food litigation listed under "deliberately not here" | Two of its orders were published on the same page |
| #1008 | "This statute has not yet produced the kind of landmark litigation that reshapes practice" | Nobody had searched for Section 135 litigation |

#1002 and #1005 are now mechanically guarded — the intro derives from the same array as the
list, and `check-judgments.py` fails when an `excluded` entry names a petition number the docket
publishes (matched on the citation number, `196 of 2001` ≡ `196/2001`, not party names, because
"Union of India" is one side of most of this file). **#1008 gets no guard**: no check can tell a
true claim about case law from a false one. It got a comment in the generator explaining the
constraint instead.

### `meta.excluded` vs `meta.coverage_notes`
New field this session. `excluded` means **absent** — considered and not published; renders under
"What is deliberately not here". `coverage_notes` means **published, read it narrowly** — renders
under "How these entries were checked". Mixing them is what shipped #1005. Documented in
`docs/judgments-standard.md`.

### The failure underneath all three
Treating "my tool didn't find it" as "it isn't findable", then writing that up as principled
restraint. It cost five real judgments (later found by searching properly), the PUCL ICDS order
(excluded because I required a term that order doesn't contain), and #1008. The user's line is
worth keeping: *"the Right to Food litigation is very famous, I am sure you will find it online."*

### Operational
- `check-fix-issues.py` and `build-fix-history.py` are **different guards over the same file**.
  Running the first and assuming it covers the second turned CI red on #1006. Run the whole
  suite, not a subset.
- **The link crawl is no longer in `ci.yml`.** As of 2026-09-08 (#1081) it lives in its own
  `link-check.yml`, daily at 07:23 UTC plus `workflow_dispatch`, `fail: true`, with `Backups/`
  excluded. It does not run on PRs at all, so a PR no longer sits at `mergeable_state: unstable`
  waiting on it. Reason: a job cancelled by `timeout-minutes` cancels the **whole run**, so the
  advisory crawl overrunning took 44 unrelated jobs with it (PR #1069, PR #1079, main run 2614 at
  30m33s) and `fail: schedule-only` could not stop that — it governs the step's exit status, not
  the run's. Do not move a job that talks to hundreds of third-party hosts back into `ci.yml`.
- The check-runs API served a stale `in_progress` for axe-core on two PRs while the real result
  had already posted as a PR comment. Cross-check the sticky comment before concluding a job hung.
- Netlify's 300s CDN cache produced a false "wrong content" reading again (POSH guide showed 2 of
  4 cases). Re-fetch with `Cache-Control: no-cache` before believing a discrepancy.

### Public finance — there is no flagship
Checked on request: `data/counts.json` says 20 flagships and none is public finance. What exists is
`101-courses/public-finance-budgeting.html` (103 slides) plus fiscal material inside the Public
Policy and Public Choice flagships. So a Fiscal Playground-style explorer would either anchor a
**new** flagship or stand on the 101 deck — not "a companion to our public finance flagship".
fiscalplayground.in (Sarthak Pradhan and Pranay Kotasthane, *Fiscal Fables*) is a hub of three
tools: income-tax spending breakdown, per-state fiscal indicators from GSDP data, and a state
budget document analyser. Its page is JS-rendered — the tool list is in an inline JS array, so a
plain fetch reads empty. **The hard part is data, not UI**: we hold none of those series.

## 2026-08-25 (later) — The Union Budget Explorer, and extracting eight years out of PDFs

`/fiscal.html` shipped, then gained a 2017-18 → 2024-25 series. The build path is worth
recording because almost none of it was guessable in advance.

### The data
- **Only the current year is a spreadsheet.** `indiabudget.gov.in` publishes 260 XLSX files for
  the live budget; every prior year is PDF only. Archive URL shapes differ by era —
  `budget2025-26/`, `budget2018-2019/`, `budget_archive/ub2010-11/` — and go back to 1999-2000.
  My first guess (`budget2025-2026`) 404s; the recent years use a **2-digit** second year.
- `data/sources/budget-at-a-glance-2026-27.xlsx` is vendored (28 KB) so the current-year
  extraction is reproducible in CI with no network. The seven PDFs are **not** committed (14 MB);
  their URL and SHA-256 live in `union-budget-history.json` → `meta.provenance`.

### PDF tooling in this sandbox
- **`pypdf` and `pdfplumber` are both unusable**: `cryptography` raises
  `pyo3_runtime.PanicException` on import. There is no `pdftotext`/`poppler` either.
- **`pymupdf` works** and does not touch `cryptography`. `pip install pymupdf`.
- `page.find_tables()` finds **nothing** in these PDFs — they have no ruled lines. Group words
  into visual rows by y-coordinate (3pt buckets) and order by x.
- Import it **lazily**. A guard that validates committed JSON must not require a PDF library;
  importing at module level failed CI, where only `openpyxl` is installed.

### What the arithmetic caught that reading never would
Every PDF year's heads summed to **2–6% more** than that document's own printed Grand Total. The
cause was one sub-row — `of which Transfer to GST Compensation Fund` — whose label wraps across
two bands *with a Hindi-only band between them*, so it read as a head in its own right while its
amount already sat inside Tax Administration. **Nothing in the text revealed this.** Two checks
now run in CI and would catch a recurrence: consecutive budgets must agree where they overlap
(each restates the prior year's estimate — 6 independent overlaps), and each year must sum to its
own published total (residuals now ±1 crore).

### Normalisation can invent facts
Merging wrapped labels by prefix is necessary — `- - Fertiliser` / `- Fertiliser` / `Fertiliser`
were three "heads" and the chart showed the subsidy vanishing and returning. But it must **refuse
to absorb a parenthesised qualifier**:
- `Agriculture and Allied Activities` → `... (Excluding PM-KISAN)` stamped a qualifier onto seven
  years whose documents say no such thing. Only the 2023-24 budget excludes PM-KISAN.
- `Home Affairs` vs `Home Affairs (including Union Territories)`: in 2021-22 those were two heads
  (₹1,12,301 + ₹56,490 cr); from 2023-24 they are one (₹1,96,872 cr). Merging draws a **63% jump
  that never happened.**
A head absent in a year is `null`, never 0, and the change column is blank for any head not
present in all eight years rather than comparing a shorter window under an eight-year header.

### A JS bug worth remembering
The page reported the history tab "unavailable" while the data sat loaded in memory. Cause: the
load callback already declared `var H = heads()` for a dropdown, and **`var` hoists to the top of
the whole callback** — so assigning the fetched data to an outer `H` landed on that local, which
was then overwritten by the head list. Symptom looked like a scope mystery: `H` truthy in the
`.then`, falsy in the renderer, `window.__H` truthy. Renamed to `HIST`. When adding a variable to
an existing callback, grep the callback for the name first.

### Honesty carried over from the docket
The Ministry's own disclaimer — *"in case of any inconsistency, pdf files will be treated as
final"* — is quoted on the page, not paraphrased. The income-tax figure is labelled a
**proportion, not a trace**: taxes on income are ~27% of Union receipts and nothing marks one
rupee as anyone's. Licensing was considered and deliberately left as attribution: GODL-India on
data.gov.in would be an explicit grant, but republishing published government figures with
attribution matches what every other explorer here already does.
